A browser workspace and FastAPI agent coordinate a local OpenAI-compatible language service with a project-internal YOLO-style oriented-object-detection endpoint that returns FAIR1M metadata-backed structured results. Two fixed FAIR1M workloads, one single-image and one serial two-image request, were repeated 20 times each and completed 20/20 attempts. Mean Full-Agent latency was 29.353 s and 60.937 s, with empirical P95 values of 31.166 s and 66.882 s. Mean detector time was 861.386 ms and 1510.920 ms, only 2.93% and 2.48% of the corresponding Full-Agent means. Profiling indicates that most visible latency occurs outside detector execution. Mean CPU utilization was 20.761% and 20.482%. A 200-ms NPU-load field averaged 100% for both workloads, but it represents a shared-accelerator software field rather than detector-only occupancy or calibrated utilization. The public evidence package provides sanitized request-level records, redacted JSON, normalized SSE examples, and scripts reproducing the reported statistics. These results establish a reproducible HIL boundary for observable satellite edge-agent orchestration, but do not establish detector accuracy, a new geolocation method, calibrated energy efficiency, or flight readiness.

</details>


### [295] [CARE-Bench: Benchmarking Patient-Facing LLM Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03731)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Cyrus Ayubcha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CARE-Bench，一个基于来源的基准测试，用于评估面向患者的医疗分诊系统。基准包含500个案例和1,059个评估前缀，将分诊定义为四标签的逐轮当前动作任务。对11个模型在无提示和最小提示协议下进行评估，发现无提示的宏F1仅31.2-50.4，虽有提示提升但仍存在大量阈值错误，正确动作是询问更多信息时仅33.5%的输出保留该步骤，表明患者分诊不是简单的提示问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向患者的医疗LLM和代理在临床接触前越来越多地回答症状问题，其关键安全问题在于用户下一步应采取什么行动，但目前缺乏系统评估这一顺序性分诊能力的基准。

Method: 构建CARE-Bench基准，从医疗对话、咨询和随访问题来源重建500个案例和1,059个患者披露前缀，采用每轮四标签当前动作任务。使用固定GPT-5.5映射器对11个模型在无提示和最小提示两种开放协议下的269个保留轮次进行编码评估。

Result: 无提示宏F1范围为31.2-50.4，较低；提示改进11个模型中10个，宏F1范围46.9-63.4，但大量阈值错误持续存在。当正确动作是询问更多信息时，仅33.5%的提示输出保留该步骤。

Conclusion: 提示工程后的持续错误表明，面向患者的分诊不是一个简单的提示问题，需要在部署前对动作时机进行显式评估。

Abstract: Patient-facing medical LLMs and agents increasingly answer symptom questions before clinician contact, where the key safety question is what action the user should take next. We introduce CARE-Bench, a source-grounded benchmark that evaluates sequential patient-facing triage as a four-label per-turn current-action task. CARE-Bench contains 500 cases and 1,059 evaluated patient-disclosure prefixes reconstructed from medical dialogue, consultation, and follow-up-question sources. We evaluate 11 models on 269 held-out rounds under unprompted and minimally prompted open-ended protocols, using a fixed GPT-5.5 mapper to code each response into the four-label action space. Unprompted macro-F1 remains low, ranging from 31.2 to 50.4. Prompting improves 10 of 11 models, with prompted macro-F1 ranging from 46.9 to 63.4, but substantial threshold errors remain. Prompted models often recommend care before needed clarification is obtained; when the correct action was to ask for more information, only 33.5% of prompted outputs preserved the step. The persistence of these errors after prompting suggests that patient-facing triage is not a simple prompting problem and supports explicit evaluation of action timing before deployment.

</details>


### [296] [Failure-Informed Image Self-Augmentation for Multimodal Large Language Model Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03733)
*Chunyang Jiang,Pingping Zhang,Yuzhi Zhao,Wenao Ma,Zhijian Hou,Mengyang Wu,Yiyang Cai,Senkang Hu,Sitong Cheng,Chi-Min Chan,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FISA框架，通过模型自身失败案例生成图像增强样本，用于多模态大语言模型的自我改进。该方法在视觉问答基准上持续提升分布内和分布外性能，且与文本自增强方法兼容，数据效率优于通用图像增强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型依赖大规模高质量标注数据，标注成本高昂。现有自增强方法侧重文本侧，图像增强未被充分探索，且常用的通用或手工变换与模型实际能力缺陷缺乏对齐，难以针对性提升模型表现。

Method: FISA框架包含三个核心步骤：(1)从模型自身失败案例出发，构造视觉上具有挑战性但答案保持不变的图像复杂化样本；(2)通过自我检查（self-examination）验证这些增强样本的实用性；(3)应用双重保真度过滤（dual fidelity filtering）以避免语义失真。

Result: 在视觉问答基准上，FISA在分布内和分布外设置下均持续提升性能。进一步实验验证了其与现有文本自增强方法的兼容性、合成样本相较通用图像增强基线具有更优的数据效率，以及所提过滤策略的实际有效性。

Conclusion: FISA证明了从模型自身失败案例出发的图像自增强是提升MLLM性能的有效途径，弥补了现有自增强方法在图像侧的空白，为构建低成本高质量训练数据提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across vision-language tasks, but their progress depends heavily on large-scale, high-quality multimodal data that are costly to annotate. Self-augmentation offers a promising alternative by enabling models to expand their own training data without external supervision. However, existing MLLM self-augmentation methods are largely text-centric, while image augmentation remains underexplored and typically relies on generic or handcrafted transformations that are weakly aligned with the model's actual incapability. We propose Failure-informed Image Self-Augmentation (\textbf{FISA}), a framework for MLLM self-improvement that constructs augmented images from the model's own failure cases. Our method generates visually challenging yet answer-preserving image complications, verifies their utility through self-examination, and applies dual fidelity filtering to avoid semantic distortion. Experiments on visual question answering benchmarks show that the proposed method consistently improves performance across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Further experiments validate the compatibility of FISA with existing textual self-augmentation approaches, the superior data efficiency of the synthesized samples over generic image augmentation baselines, and the practical effectiveness of the proposed filtering strategy.

</details>


### [297] [AgenticECO: An Agentic Framework for ECO on 3D Integrated Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03738)
*Shuo Ren,Yaohui Han,Libo Shen,Zhiqiang Jia,Rongliang Fu,Bei Yu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgenticECO，一个基于证据门控的工具调用代理工作流，用于3D-IC的ECO（工程变更单），结合EcoRoute最小扰动布线层，在修复可归因于编辑的前提下，显著提升缺陷修复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩尔定律放缓推动三维集成，但3D-IC流程中布线后的键合级缺陷没有2D对应物，且布线后ECO仍依赖手动、专家经验。现有"编辑-全重布线"惯例使修复与路由器变动纠缠不清，无法将签核指标归因于触发修复的编辑。

Method: 设计AgenticECO（证据门控的工具调用代理工作流）运行于开源TaiWei流程，并与EcoRoute（最小扰动ECO布线层）配对，后者驱动未修改的固定路由器，使每次修复可归因于其编辑。

Result: 在九个匹配的自然缺陷案例、相同预算下，AgenticECO清除七个（全重布线和库存修复均仅清除两个），平均扰动0.66%，零时钟网络触碰；跨骨干网重运行在同一密封契约下清除全部九例。对照实验验证修复移动在保留下的必要性、占用感知选择带来合法落点、收紧时钟下最小扰动决定接受与否。三个预注册视觉研究定位像素工具的边界，盲诊断精确恢复所有保留的注入缺陷，是唯一零错误编辑的实验组。

Conclusion: 所有接受的结果均通过布线、重新提取、最大/最小时序、DRC和结构等价门控验证；代码、环境和逐次审计产物已作为补充材料发布，表明该工作流具备可复现性和实用性。

Abstract: As Moore's law slows, the industry is turning to three-dimensional integration; yet in merged 3D-IC flows, routed designs expose bond-level defects with no 2D analogue, and post-route engineering change orders (ECO) remain manual, expertise-bound work. Worse, the standard edit-then-fully-reroute practice entangles a repair with router churn, so a signoff number cannot be attributed to the edit that motivated it. We present AgenticECO, an evidence-gated tool-using agent workflow for 3D-IC ECO on the open-source TaiWei flow, paired with EcoRoute, a minimal-disturbance ECO-routing layer that drives the unmodified pinned router so a repair is attributable to its edit. Across nine matched natural defect cases under identical budgets, AgenticECO clears seven versus two for both full reroute and stock repair, at 0.66\% mean disturbance over cleared cases and zero clock nets touched, and a cross-backbone rerun under the same sealed contract clears all nine. Controlled studies show that the repair moves are necessary under preservation, that occupancy-aware choice buys legal landings rather than repair success, and that under tightened clocks minimal disturbance flips accept versus reject. Three preregistered visual studies localize the pixel instrument's edge to contested landing sites, and a preregistered blind diagnostic exactly restores every held-out injected defect, the only arm with zero wrong edits. Every accepted result passes routing, fresh extraction, max/min timing, DRC, and structural-equivalence gates. Code, environment, and per-episode audit artifacts are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [298] [MissClick: Exploiting Digit-Serialized Coordinates to Attack GUI Grounding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03740)
*Yu Ran,Wentao Zhao,Xin Zhang,Yi Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MissClick，一种针对GUI视觉接地模型的自盒对抗攻击方法。该攻击利用坐标生成过程中数字token的数值与位值结构特性，提出两种攻击目标：MissClick-U（软坐标位移，用于非定向攻击）和MissClick-T（按位加权目标数字损失，用于定向攻击）。在OS-Atlas和UGround模型上，跨桌面、网页和移动平台，非定向攻击成功率分别达75.07%和72.93%，定向攻击成功率分别达44.86%和62.67%，显著超越现有攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI视觉接地模型将屏幕坐标生成为数字token序列，但其坐标生成过程的安全性影响被严重忽视。作者观察到坐标数字token的数值和位值结构特性（如百位数字变化1导致坐标变化100），这使坐标输出不应被视为普通文本，而应利用其数值结构设计攻击目标。同时，非定向与定向攻击的成功条件不同，需要针对性的攻击目标设计。

Method: 提出MissClick白盒对抗攻击，包含两个目标特定组件：（1）MissClick-U通过最大化软坐标位移实现非定向破坏；（2）MissClick-T通过最小化按位加权目标数字损失实现定向劫持。该方法利用坐标输出的数值和位值结构而非将其视为普通文本序列。

Result: 在OS-Atlas和UGround模型上，跨桌面、网页、移动平台，MissClick-U的非定向成功率分别达75.07%和72.93%（比现有方法高16.62和30.72个百分点），MissClick-T的定向成功率分别达44.86%和62.67%（比现有方法高31.73和47.06个百分点）。攻击目标对比表明，软坐标位移最适合非定向攻击，按位加权目标数字优化最适合定向攻击。

Conclusion: 攻击目标的选择对两种攻击目标（非定向与定向）具有不同的最优偏好，验证了针对GUI接地模型设计的数值与位值感知攻击目标的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Recent GUI visual grounding models generate screen coordinates as sequences of digit tokens that are parsed into numerical values and mapped to executable clicks. The security implications of this coordinate generation process have been largely overlooked. We observe that each coordinate digit is predicted as a categorical token, yet after parsing, changing a hundreds-place digit by one changes the corresponding numerical coordinate component by 100 units, which can induce a large displacement of the executed click. This observation motivates attack objectives that account for the numerical and place-value structure of coordinate outputs rather than treating them as ordinary text. Moreover, untargeted and targeted attacks impose different success conditions--displacing the click outside the correct region versus into an attacker-specified region--and therefore benefit from different objectives. We propose MissClick, a simple and effective white-box adversarial attack with two goal-specific objectives: MissClick-U maximizes soft-coordinate displacement for untargeted disruption, while MissClick-T minimizes a place-weighted target-digit loss for targeted hijacking. Compared with existing attacks against GUI grounding models on OS-Atlas and UGround across desktop, web, and mobile platforms, MissClick-U achieves untargeted success rates of 75.07\% and 72.93\% (+16.62 and +30.72 pp), and MissClick-T achieves targeted success rates of 44.86\% and 62.67\% (+31.73 and +47.06 pp). Attack objective comparison further shows that soft-coordinate displacement yields the highest untargeted attack success rate, whereas place-weighted target-digit optimization yields the highest targeted attack success rate, revealing distinct objective preferences for the two attack goals.

</details>


### [299] [Agents Catching Agents: Shortcut Cascades and Benchmark Gaming in Clinical Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03744)
*Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Agastya Munnangi,Aldo Marzullo,Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Quang Bui,Mohammad Shahin,Armaan Grewal,Emmanuel Paul Kwesiga,Anqi Peter Li,Josephine Nanyonjo,Aaditya Panchal,Arshnoor Bhutani,Nikhil Jaiswal,Milit S. Patel,Maximin Lange,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLM）委员会在临床决策支持中是否会被"捷径"（benchmark奖励但临床医生会忽略的线索）所欺骗。研究发现，Gemini委员会在孤立情况下能抵抗这些线索（翻转率仅5-16%），但社会性合理的捷径会传播：当两个同伴断言同一个错误答案时，被测者的采纳率高达38%。三种监督代理中，只有独立重新查询被测者的"裁判"能有效检测（文本精度77-88%，假阳性率13-21%），而门控和同源法官各有缺陷。结论是"社会合理性"是委员会被攻破的关键，只有独立于自我报告的裁判才能捕捉它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持正走向多代理LLM委员会在共享工作空间上商议的模式。作者想探究这种委员会是否会被"捷径"所欺骗——即基准测试奖励但临床医生会忽略的线索，以确保这类系统的可靠性和安全性。

Method: 在7个队列、6个公开数据集（涵盖文本MedQA-USMLE、MedMCQA、MIMIC-CXR报告；影像NIH ChestX-ray14、MIMIC-CXR-JPG、CheXpert；表格ICU记录SUPPORT2）上，测试Gemini委员会对孤立线索的抗性、社会性捷径（同伴断言、假预筛标志）的传播效应，并比较三种监督代理（门控、同源法官、独立裁判）的检测能力。

Result: 委员会在孤立情况下抵抗线索（翻转5-16%），但社会性捷径显著传播：两个同伴断言同一错误答案时采纳率38%，假预筛标志同样有效。门控无法区分采纳与诚实一致（假阳性率100%）；同源法官在文本上效果优异（精度100%、召回93%）但在影像上失效；独立裁判可迁移到影像（精度77-88%，假阳性率13-21%）。线索的视觉显著性增加三倍不影响传染，而第二个同伴声音使传染增加50%。只有1/10文本和1/134影像漂移者能说出他们移动所朝向的rubric。

Conclusion: 委员会被攻破的关键是"社会合理性"线索而非单纯的视觉线索，且这种欺骗近乎无声。只有独立于自我报告的裁判代理能够有效捕捉这种采纳行为，为构建更安全的临床LLM委员会系统提供了重要设计启示。

Abstract: Clinical decision support is moving toward committees of language-model agents deliberating on a shared workspace. We ask whether such committees can be gamed by shortcuts, cues a benchmark rewards but a clinician would ignore. Across seven cohorts on six public datasets spanning text (MedQA-USMLE, MedMCQA, MIMIC-CXR reports), imaging (NIH ChestX-ray14, MIMIC-CXR-JPG, CheXpert) and tabular ICU records (SUPPORT2), Gemini committees resist these cues in isolation (flip 5-16%), yet a socially plausible shortcut spreads: when two peers assert the same wrong answer, the holdout under test adopts it in 38% of cases, as does a false "pre-screen" system flag, on both capability tiers. Of three oversight agents, a gate cannot separate adoption from honest agreement (false-positive rate 100%); a same-lineage judge reading only the transcript flags adoption on text (precision 100%, recall 93%) but collapses onto the gate in imaging; a referee that privately re-queries the holdout transfers to imaging (77-88% precision, 13-21% false-positive rate). Tripling a cue's visual salience does not move contagion, whereas a second peer voice raises it by half again. Gaming a hidden rubric is near-silent: only 1/10 text and 1/134 imaging drifters name the rubric they moved toward. What games a committee is social plausibility, and only a referee independent of self-report catches it. Code: https://github.com/criticaldata/benchmaxxing

</details>


### [300] [Risky Business: Measuring The Faithfulness-Safety Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03745)
*Dominik Meier,Luca Joshua Francis,Marco Bernhard Kaiser,Terry Ruas,Jan Philip Wahle,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型推理模型（LRM）中监控所需的"忠实性"与安全所需的"拒绝不安全推理"之间存在对齐张力，提出了基于替换的干预技术TRR和人类编写的HazMart数据集，并通过表征操控（Representation Steering）缓解这一矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理虽为模型监控提供了窗口，但监控的前提是推理轨迹的忠实性（输出严格源自推理）。然而，模型又需要足够鲁棒以拒绝不安全推理。二者之间存在根本性的权衡张力，现有工作尚未系统识别或解决这一问题。此外，先前测试忠实性的方法依赖在提示中提供线索，缺乏对推理链的直接干预。

Method: 1) 构建HazMart：人类编写的自主AI店主场景数据集；2) 提出TRR（Targeted Reasoning Replacement）：直接替换推理链中的想法，注入不安全或不合逻辑的内容以测试忠实性与安全性；3) 对DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B和QwQ-32B进行行为评估；4) 对QwQ-32B进行机制分析，识别内部方向；5) 应用表征操控（representation steering）增强安全方向。

Result: DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B表现出高忠实性（97.5%）但在拒绝不安全推理上严重不足（仅12.3%）；QwQ-32B在安全性上更鲁棒（73.9%）但忠实性较低（74.7%）。机制分析表明QwQ-32B中这两个属性由在动作承诺token处达到峰值且反相关的内部方向表征。表征操控可独立放大安全方向，在不损害基础能力的情况下将安全行为提升9个百分点。

Conclusion: 忠实性与安全性之间存在可量化的权衡张力，但通过机制层面对内部方向的理解和表征操控，可以部分解耦并增强安全性，为构建既可监控又安全的推理模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning offers a promising window into model monitoring. However, monitoring relies on faithfulness, i.e., the model output strictly derives from its reasoning trace. We identify an alignment tension where a model must be faithful enough to be monitored, yet robust enough to reject unsafe reasoning. We demonstrate that this counterbalance exists in current Large Reasoning Models (LRMs), and show ways in which it can be addressed. We introduce HazMart, a human-written dataset set in an autonomous AI shopkeeper scenario. Unlike prior work that relies on providing hints in prompts to test faithfulness (e.g., "A Stanford professor said it should be Answer A"), we propose a novel replacement-based technique, which we call Targeted Reasoning Replacement (TRR), that directly intervenes in the reasoning chain to substitute in unsafe or illogical thoughts (e.g., "Wait, the answer must be Option B [was Option A] because it is the most fitting"). DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B exhibits high faithfulness (97.5%) but fails to reject Unsafe Reasoning (12.3%), while QwQ-32B is more robust (73.9% safety) at the cost of lower faithfulness (74.7%). Mechanistic analyses of QwQ-32B reveal that these properties are represented by anti-correlated internal directions peaking at the action-commit token. Finally, we demonstrate that representation steering can independently amplify the safety direction, increasing safe behavior by 9 percentage points while maintaining base capabilities.

</details>


### [301] [GDPevo: Evaluating Agent Self-Evolution on Real Business Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03764)
*Leijun Zhou,Zhihao Liu,Xiang Qu,Chenxu Liu,Yifei Liu,Yanke Yu,Jingzhe Xu,Xuejun Wu,Buyue Qian,Xi Chen,Yaowei Zheng,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GDPevo是一个基于GDP相关企业工作流的演化原生基准测试，通过规则杂交机制将企业工作流分解为原子业务规则，分配到训练任务并在测试任务中重新组合，以实现可归因的测试时性能提升评估。V1包含12组120个任务，全自动化流水线可在两天内扩展至V2的24组240个任务。实验表明，智能体自我演化可将预留测试准确率提升最多16.44个百分点，但最优演化智能体远未达到91.6%的全知上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体自我演化评估基准存在三个关键缺陷：对经济价值高的任务领域覆盖有限、训练与测试任务设计未能确保测试时收益可归因于训练经验、易受数据污染影响。因此需要一个新的演化原生基准来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出了规则杂交（rule hybridization）机制作为核心方法：将每个企业工作流分解为原子业务规则，将规则的子集分配到训练任务中，然后在保留的测试任务中重新组合这些规则。同时构建了全自动化的数据流水线，可快速扩展任务规模。基准覆盖CRM、ERP、金融、医疗、法律和数据中心化工作流。

Result: 在GDPevo上评估了四个智能体（每个包含harness和模型）和四种监督类型。自我演化一致地提升了预留准确率，最高提升16.44个百分点。但最佳演化智能体距离91.6%的全知oracle上限仍有明显差距，表明当前智能体的自我演化能力尚未充分实现。

Conclusion: GDPevo提供了首个针对企业工作流的演化原生基准，其全自动化流水线能快速扩展并有效应对数据污染问题。实验证明自我演化确实能提升智能体性能，但当前智能体的自我演化能力距离完全实现仍有很大空间。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution updates an agent's persistent state from prior experience and reuses it to solve related tasks more effectively. Evaluating self-evolution is difficult: existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of economically valuable task domains, do not always design training and test tasks such that test-time gains can be attributed to training experience, and remain vulnerable to data contamination. We present GDPevo, an evolution-native benchmark grounded in GDP-related enterprise workflows, together with the fully automated data pipeline that generates it. Its core mechanism, rule hybridization, decomposes each enterprise workflow into atomic business rules, distributes subsets of these rules across training tasks, and recombines them in held-out test tasks so that test-time gains are attributable. GDPevo spans CRM, ERP, finance, healthcare, legal, and data-centric workflows. Its V1 release contains 120 tasks in 12 groups, with five training and five held-out test tasks per group. Full automation enables the pipeline to expand the suite to 240 tasks in 24 groups (V2) within two days, providing a practical response to contamination. Using GDPevo, we evaluate four agents, each comprising a harness and a model, under four supervision types. Self-evolution consistently improves held-out accuracy by up to 16.44 percentage points. But the best evolved agents remain far below the fully informed oracle ceiling of 91.6%, indicating that the self-evolution ability of current agents remains far from fully realized. We publicly release the pipeline, benchmark, and full evaluation results at https://github.com/Prism-Shadow/GDPevo.

</details>


### [302] [Computing Actual Causes for Neural Network Predictions under Structured Causal Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03772)
*Jannick Strobel,Muqsit Azeem,Stefan Leue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用 Halpern-Pearl (HP) 实际因果关系来形式化神经网络预测的解释，通过布尔结构因果模型（SCMs）建模输入依赖关系，并利用边界传播和分支定界技术高效计算所有最小实际原因，可扩展到搜索空间达 2.3×10^13 的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释方法（如特征归因或最小充分集）通常假设输入特征相互独立，当输入存在结构化依赖时会产生误导性解释。

Method: 将解释形式化为 Halpern-Pearl 实际原因，使用布尔结构因果模型建模输入依赖，通过边界传播（bound propagation）和分支定界（branch-and-bound）技术计算 HP 原因，并提供完备性和最小性的形式化保证。

Result: 在可扩展性上大幅超越暴力搜索和 ILP 基线；在搜索空间达 2.3×10^13 个候选（原因，反事实）对、最多 28 个节点的 SCM 上，能在每个实例 180 秒预算内计算所有最小实际原因；随图规模增大优于启发式搜索。案例分析显示忽略输入依赖会使报告的原因数膨胀，其中 14.9% 在 SCM 下是虚假的。

Conclusion: 通过 HP 因果关系框架和高效的优化算法，可以在考虑输入依赖的情况下提供可靠且可扩展的神经网络解释，同时揭示了忽略输入依赖会导致大量虚假解释的问题。

Abstract: Explaining the predictions of neural networks is a central challenge in trustworthy AI. Existing explanation methods, such as those based on feature attribution or minimal sufficient sets, typically treat input features as independent, which can yield misleading explanations when inputs exhibit structured dependencies. We address this by formalizing explanations as Halpern-Pearl (HP) actual causes, modeling input dependencies using Boolean Structural Causal Models (SCMs). We compute HP causes by applying bound propagation and branch-and-bound techniques, while providing formal guarantees of completeness and minimality. Our experiments show that we substantially outperform brute-force and ILP baselines in scalability, and outperform heuristic search as graph size grows, computing all minimal actual causes on instances with search spaces of up to $2.3\times10^{13}$ candidate (cause, contingency) pairs, on SCMs with up to 28 nodes, within a 180s per-instance budget. In a case study, we further show that ignoring input dependencies inflates the number of reported causes, 14.9% of which are spurious under our SCM.

</details>


### [303] [KnowHal: A Knowledge-Driven Benchmark for Comprehensive Multimodal Hallucination Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03782)
*Ruihan Li,Jiyang Tan,Kailin Jiang,Huining Li,Hengyang Lu,Yu Huang,Qian Li,Yuntao Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KnowHal基准，将知识幻觉显式纳入多模态大模型幻觉评估，覆盖实体、属性、关系、知识四个维度的统一评估框架，包含1800个样本和成对正负问题设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准主要关注实体、属性和关系幻觉，知识相关失败通常被单独研究，缺乏横跨不同幻觉维度的统一评估框架，无法全面评估MLLM的幻觉问题。

Method: 构建KnowHal基准，覆盖四个维度（实体、属性、关系、知识）。采用成对正负问题设计，在共享图片和实体上构造对照问题，通过半自动化流程（LLM辅助、CLIP过滤、人工验证）构建1800个样本，涵盖10个领域和50个类别。

Result: 评估了14个代表性MLLM，发现知识维度对几乎所有模型都是最大挑战；大多数模型在负问题上表现显著下降，暴露了对错误前提的鲁棒性有限。

Conclusion: KnowHal通过统一四个幻觉维度并结合成对问题设计，填补了现有评估框架的空白，实现了对MLLM幻觉更全面的评估。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a critical challenge for developing trustworthy Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). While existing benchmarks mainly focus on entity, attribute, and relation hallucinations, knowledge-related failures are often investigated separately, lacking a unified evaluation framework across different hallucination dimensions. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{KnowHal}, a benchmark that explicitly incorporates knowledge hallucination into multimodal hallucination evaluation spanning four dimensions: entity, attribute, relation, and knowledge. KnowHal constructs paired positive and negative questions over shared images and entities, enabling controlled comparisons among perceptual errors, knowledge-related errors, and false-premise acceptance. The benchmark contains 1,800 samples across 10 domains and 50 categories, constructed through a semi-automated pipeline combining LLM assistance, CLIP-based filtering, and human verification. We evaluate 14 representative MLLMs on KnowHal and conduct extensive analyses. Results show that the knowledge dimension consistently presents the greatest challenge for nearly all evaluated models, while most models exhibit substantial performance degradation on negative questions, revealing limited robustness to false premises. By unifying four hallucination dimensions with paired question design, KnowHal addresses an important gap in existing evaluation frameworks and enables a more comprehensive assessment of hallucinations in MLLMs.

</details>


### [304] [Does Forgetting Transfer Across Modalities? A Real-World Benchmark for Cross-Modal Knowledge Unlearning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03791)
*Chunlin Liu,Junnian Chen,Haitong Jiang,Jianyu Zhao,Yingsen Pang,Jingchen Li,Jiabiao He,Youming Lu,Jinhe Bi,Yuntao Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UNLINK-VL，一个用于视觉语言模型（VLM）跨模态知识遗忘的真实世界基准。该基准在原始遗忘/保留语料不可得的场景下，选取视觉可识别实体作为遗忘目标，并关联图像与Wikidata中的一跳和多跳事实。实验发现跨模态迁移存在显著不对称性：多模态遗忘在文本评估下仍有效，而纯文本遗忘难以迁移到视觉和跨模态场景，说明仅依赖单一模态评估会高估VLM知识遗忘的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM与LLM一样可能记忆敏感、受版权保护或有害的知识。现有遗忘研究主要集中在单一模态内，虽然已有工作开始探索跨模态一致性，但真实世界知识的跨模态迁移遗忘研究仍不充分。

Method: 构建UNLINK-VL基准：(1)在post-hoc遗忘设定下（原始遗忘/保留语料不可得）；(2)选取视觉可识别的真实世界实体作为遗忘目标；(3)关联图像及Wikidata中的一跳和多跳事实；(4)包含四个互补子集：直接遗忘评估、关系知识传播评估、非目标知识保留评估、语义等价查询鲁棒性评估；(5)分别在纯文本和多模态遗忘设置下训练模型，并在文本、视觉和跨模态场景下评估。

Result: 实验揭示了跨模态迁移的显著不对称性：多模态遗忘在文本评估下保持有效，而纯文本遗忘难以迁移到视觉和跨模态场景。同时，所评估的方法基本保持了模型的通用能力。

Conclusion: 仅依赖模态内评估（尤其是纯文本评估）会显著高估VLM知识遗忘的有效性，强调了跨模态遗忘与评估的必要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs), like Large Language Models (LLMs), may memorize sensitive, copyrighted, or harmful knowledge from their pretraining corpora. Removing such knowledge is essential for building trustworthy AI systems. However, existing studies primarily focus on forgetting within individual modalities. Although recent work has begun to explore cross-modal consistency in unlearning, the cross-modal transfer of real-world knowledge unlearning remains insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce UNLINK-VL, a real-world benchmark for cross-modal knowledge unlearning in VLMs. Under a post-hoc unlearning setting in which the original forget and retain corpora are unavailable, UNLINK-VL selects visually identifiable real-world entities as unlearning targets and associates them with corresponding images and one-hop and multi-hop facts derived from Wikidata. The benchmark comprises four complementary subsets that evaluate direct forgetting of target knowledge, the propagation of forgetting through relational knowledge, the preservation of related non-target knowledge, and robustness to semantically equivalent queries. We train models under text-only and multimodal unlearning settings and evaluate forgetting effectiveness and retained utility across textual, visual, and cross-modal scenarios. Extensive experiments reveal a pronounced asymmetry in cross-modal transfer: multimodal unlearning remains effective under textual evaluation, whereas text-only unlearning transfers poorly to visual and cross-modal scenarios. Meanwhile, the evaluated methods largely preserve the models' general capabilities. These findings demonstrate that relying solely on intra-modal evaluation, particularly text-only evaluation, may substantially overestimate the effectiveness of knowledge unlearning in VLMs, underscoring the need for cross-modal unlearning and evaluation.

</details>


### [305] [LatentGuard: Efficient and Inspectable Latent Reasoning for LLM Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03838)
*Zhinan Liu,Jie Li,Mingyu Kang,Jiayi Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentGuard，一个高效且可检查的安全防护框架，通过阶段性课程学习将文本推理逐步压缩为紧凑的潜在状态，直接在连续表示上预测安全判定，同时通过辅助解码器按需生成审计伪影以保持可检查性。LatentGuard-8B相比GuardReasoner-8B提升了加权F1分数（83.95→84.91），且关键路径推理代价从生成268.56个推理令牌降至1.60个潜在推理令牌，审计解码器达到85.75的审计效用分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于推理的防护模型能提升LLM安全性，但为每次交互解码显式推理会使部署成本高昂。虽然潜在推理方法通过将推理移入连续状态减少令牌生成，但该方法在安全审核领域尚未被充分探索，且缺乏部署所需的检查界面。

Method: 提出LatentGuard框架，采用（1）阶段性课程学习，逐步将任务对齐的文本推理压缩为紧凑的潜在状态；（2）直接从连续表示预测安全判定；（3）设置独立的辅助解码器，按需生成紧凑的审计伪影，将推理生成移出标准推理路径以保持检查能力。

Result: LatentGuard-8B将加权F1从83.95提升至84.91（超越GuardReasoner-8B），关键路径推理代价从268.56个生成推理令牌降至1.60个潜在推理令牌，审计解码器达到85.75的审计效用分数。

Conclusion: LatentGuard展示了在保持可检查性的同时实现高效LLM安全防护的可行路径，为可部署的防护模型提供了高效且可检查的解决方案。

Abstract: Reasoning-based guard models improve LLM safeguards, but decoding explicit rationales for every interaction makes them costly to deploy. Although latent-reasoning methods reduce token generation by moving reasoning into continuous states, they remain underexplored for safety moderation and lack an inspection interface for deployment. In this paper, we propose LatentGuard, an efficient and inspectable safeguard framework that brings continuous latent reasoning to guard models. LatentGuard uses a staged curriculum to progressively compress task-aligned textual rationales into compact latent states, enabling safety verdicts to be predicted directly from continuous representations. To preserve inspectability, an isolated auxiliary decoder generates compact audit artifacts on demand, keeping rationale generation off the standard inference path. Experiments show that LatentGuard-8B improves mean weighted F1 from 83.95 to 84.91 over GuardReasoner-8B, while reducing critical-path reasoning cost from 268.56 generated rationale tokens to 1.60 latent reasoning tokens. Its audit decoder achieves an audit utility score of 85.75, demonstrating an efficient and inspectable path toward deployable LLM safeguards.

</details>


### [306] [Oilbird: Training-Free Speculative Decoding with Keys the Verifier Already Computes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03839)
*Tao Jin,Phuong Minh Nguyen,Zhenzhu Yan,Teeradaj Racharak,Naoya Inoue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Oilbird，一种训练无关的推测解码方法，通过增加基于隐藏状态的语义草稿源来弥补精确匹配草稿器的不足，在工具调用场景下显著提升接受长度和解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练无关推测解码通过精确后缀匹配上下文来生成草稿，但在工具调用场景下会漏掉池中已有的正确草稿——因为请求几乎重复所有内容，仅有个别值是新生成的，且一个被拒绝的token会丢弃其后的正确延续。问题根源在于寻址而非覆盖：最强精确匹配草稿器遗漏的内容中约有一半其实已在池中但无法通过精确匹配访问到。

Method: 提出第二种语义草稿源：利用验证器在每<unk>个已提交token处已计算好的隐藏状态对同一池进行重新键控（re-keying），并通过合并机制使其嵌入现有词法草稿器的树结构中，实现两个草稿源的协同工作。

Result: 在三个已发表的草稿器中，匹配池和预算条件下接受长度提升24-29%。Oilbird在API-Bank上达到4.4倍自回归解码加速，对比最强训练无关基线为3.9倍，EAGLE-3为2.0倍。

Conclusion: 通过语义寻址补充精确匹配寻址，可以显著改善训练无关推测解码在工具调用等高重复场景下的性能，且无需额外训练即可获得接近甚至超越训练方法的加速效果。

Abstract: Training-free speculative decoding drafts by matching an exact suffix of the context against a pool of earlier context. That lookup misses correct drafts already in the pool, most visibly on tool-calling traffic, where a request repeats almost everything but the few values minted for it, and where one rejected token discards the correct continuation behind it. We diagnose the failure position by position across ten benchmarks and find it to be a problem of addressing rather than of coverage: on our densest tool-calling benchmark, about half of what the strongest exact-match drafter misses is present in the pool yet unreachable by exact matching. We therefore propose a second, semantic draft source: the same pool, re-keyed by the hidden state the verifier has already computed at each committed token, together with a merge that lets it ride inside an existing lexical drafter's tree. In three published drafters, at matched pool and budget, it lifts accepted length by 24-29%. Oilbird reaches 4.4x autoregressive decoding speed on API-Bank, against 3.9x for the strongest training-free baseline in our harness and 2.0x for EAGLE-3.

</details>


### [307] [MAFIA: Query-Only Memory Attacks via Probing and Factual Injection against Audited LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03844)
*Jiaming Chen,Yisen Gao,Yanping Li,Zifan Liu,Yumeng Zhang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAFIA框架，一种仅基于查询的存储投毒攻击方法，通过记忆探测和事实注入绕过审计机制，在大型良性记忆池和高检索竞争环境下实现高达90.7%的攻击成功率，同时将审计检测率从83.3%压制到不超过7.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强的LLM智能体依赖丰富的上下文进行长时程推理，但其记忆模块暴露了恶意记录的攻击面。现有的仅查询攻击在两种现实场景中失效：(1)大型良性记忆池导致检索竞争力不足；(2)主动输入审计使恶意内容被检测。因此需要一种能同时应对高检索竞争性和严格语义检查的攻击方法。

Method: MAFIA框架包含两个核心组件：(1)放置策略——通过记忆探测、预算分配和调度确保注入的记忆记录在检索中具有竞争力；(2)载荷设计——使用紧凑的事实伪装层绕过审计，在保持恶意效果的同时维持高语义相似度。

Result: 大量评估表明MAFIA实现了最高90.7%的攻击成功率，并将审计检测率从峰值83.3%压制到最多7.4%，揭示了大模型智能体记忆系统中的关键安全漏洞。

Conclusion: MAFIA证明了在现实场景中针对记忆增强LLM智能体的记忆投毒攻击是可行且有效的，现有记忆系统的安全防护（审计机制和检索竞争）不足以抵御精心设计的恶意注入，亟需更稳健的记忆安全防御措施。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents rely on rich context for long-horizon reasoning and acting, yet their memory modules expose a persistent attack surface for malicious records, making the study of memory poisoning threats imperative. However, existing query-only attacks often fail to remain effective in two realistic and prevalent settings: large-scale benign memory pools and active input auditing. Consequently, current approaches fall short when facing the dual challenges of high retrieval competitiveness and rigorous semantic checks. To overcome these limitations, we propose MAFIA, a query-only Memory Attack framework via probing and Factual Injection against Audit, tailored to this extended threat model. Specifically, MAFIA introduces: (1) a placement strategy that ensures retrieval-competitive injection via memory probing, budget allocation, and scheduling; and (2) a payload design that bypasses audits using compact factual cloaks, preserving malicious effects while maintaining high semantic similarity. Extensive evaluations reveal that MAFIA achieves up to a 90.7% attack success rate while suppressing audit detection from a peak of 83.3% to at most 7.4%, exposing critical vulnerabilities across agentic memory systems. Code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/JiamingChen1234/MAFIA.

</details>


### [308] [ContinualSkillBench: Can LLM Agents Truly Evolve Their Capabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03874)
*Tianyi Guan,Yiding Wang,Haotong Yang,Siyuan Cao,Shirui Liu,Yi Hu,Jiaqi Li,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ContinualSkillBench，一个用于评估大语言模型在上下文中持续学习技能能力的动态基准框架，涵盖五个领域、500个相互关联的子任务。实验发现顺序执行能提升性能，但提升幅度因模型和领域而异；上下文学习与显式技能维护效果相当；能力较弱的模型倾向于积累更庞大且碎片化的技能库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体框架虽然为LLM配备了外部技能库来解决复杂任务，但现有系统能否有效进化技能、以及这些技能是否真正提升任务解决能力，仍然不明确。缺乏一个系统的评估框架来研究这类上下文持续技能学习问题。

Method: 构建ContinualSkillBench动态评估框架，覆盖五个代表性领域（每个领域100个相互关联的子任务），任务按难度递增排序并包含跨任务技能复用机会。通过对比顺序执行、显式技能维护和纯上下文学习等不同策略的性能表现，来分析持续的技能进化机制。

Result: 实验表明：1) 顺序执行普遍提升性能，但提升幅度因模型和领域差异显著；2) 上下文学习与显式技能维护在平均性能上相当，表明大部分提升来自对先前上下文和反馈的适应；3) 显式技能在需要可复用程序或精确输出的任务上有选择性优势；4) 能力较弱的模型倾向于积累更大、更碎片化的任务特定技能集合。

Conclusion: 当前基于上下文的技能进化机制能够支持持续的适应性，但仍然难以将经验一致性地巩固为稳健且可迁移的技能，这表明该领域仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Modern agent frameworks equip large language models with external skill libraries to solve complex tasks. However, it remains unclear whether these systems can effectively evolve their skills and whether the resulting skills improve task-solving capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce ContinualSkillBench, a dynamic evaluation framework for in-context continual skill learning. It covers five representative domains, each containing 100 interconnected subtasks ordered by increasing difficulty and opportunities for cross-task skill reuse. Our experiments show that sequential execution generally improves performance, but the gains vary substantially across models and domains. Moreover, in-context learning performs comparably to explicit skill maintenance on average, suggesting that much of the improvement arises from adaptation to prior context and feedback rather than reusable skill abstraction alone. Explicit skills nevertheless provide selective benefits for tasks requiring reusable procedures or precise outputs. We further find that less capable models tend to accumulate larger, more fragmented collections of task-specific skills. These findings show that current in-context skill evolution mechanisms can support continual adaptation, but still struggle to consistently consolidate experience into robust and transferable skills.

</details>


### [309] [Intertemporal Preference Steering in Qwen3 via Contrastive Activation Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03892)
*Michal Mráz,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Qwen3-32B大语言模型中时间视野的线性表征，通过对比线性探针识别短期与长期方向，并利用对比激活加法控制模型的时间相关偏好、推荐和能力。核心发现是时间视野方向可通过简单对比探针识别，并用于双向大幅改变模型偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在涉及延迟成本和收益的建议中需要处理时间维度，但模型的跨期偏好是否可测量、可操控仍是开放问题。理解并控制模型的时间视野对长期规划能力和AI安全至关重要。

Method: 在Qwen3-32B的残差流上训练对比线性探针（短期vs长期），用于教师强制的时间选择任务；采用对比激活加法（CAA）进行steering，在保留的二元时间选择任务、分布外货币跨期选择任务和TravelPlanner能力基准上进行评估。

Result: 时间视野方向能被简单对比线性探针识别，并用于steering在两个方向上大幅改变模型偏好。在分布外货币选择任务中（变化奖励大小和延迟），steering显著双向改变模型对即时较小奖励与延迟较大奖励的无差异阈值。中等程度的时序steering还能提升规划相关能力指标。

Conclusion: 模型的跨期偏好是可测量且可操控的，这为需要处理延迟收益和成本建议的AI系统以及长时程规划相关的安全问题提供了重要启示。

Abstract: We study linear representations of temporal horizon in the large language model Qwen3-32B and use them to change the model's time-related preferences, recommendations, and capabilities. We train contrastive linear probes on teacher-forced temporal-choice answers to find a short-term versus long-term direction in the model's residual stream, and evaluate contrastive activation-addition steering on a held-out binary temporal-choice task, an out-of-distribution monetary intertemporal-choice task, and a TravelPlanner capability benchmark. The central result is that temporal-horizon directions can be identified with simple contrastive linear probes and then used for steering to induce large, bidirectional preference changes. On an out-of-distribution monetary choice task that varies reward size and delay, steering strongly shifts the model's indifference threshold between smaller-sooner and larger-later rewards in both directions. We further show improvements on a planning-related capability metric under moderate temporal steering. These results suggest that model intertemporal preferences are measurable and steerable, which is relevant for AI systems that give advice involving delayed costs and benefits, and for safety questions about long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [310] [When Efficiency Becomes Fragility: Exploiting Dynamic Routing Vulnerabilities in Adaptive UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03902)
*Shaofeng Liang,Runwei Guan,Wenshuo Chen,Jiemin Wu,Bowen Tian,Haozhe Jia,Kaishen Yuan,Songning Lai,Daizong Liu,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示了自适应Transformer跟踪器中计算路径决策的Lipschitz奇异性这一结构性安全缺陷，并基于此提出对抗路径反转（API）框架，通过微小的扰动精确操控门控决策，迫使推理路径发生改变，从而破坏模型表征能力，实现了更隐蔽、更有效、更快速的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机平台的资源约束推动了自适应Transformer跟踪器的发展，这类模型通过输入依赖的动态路由架构在精度和效率之间取得平衡。然而，现有研究忽视了这种计算按需灵活性背后的安全漏洞：计算路径决策的Lipschitz奇异性导致系统在离散跳层决策边界处具有无界局部Lipschitz常数，微小输入扰动即可被放大并引发推理拓扑的剧烈变化，构成一个可直接利用的新型攻击面。

Method: 作者首先在理论上形式化刻画了自适应跟踪架构中计算路径决策的Lipschitz奇异性，将其识别为可被攻击者直接利用的新攻击面。在此基础上提出对抗路径反转（API）框架，通过生成难以察觉的扰动来精确操控门控模块的决策，迫使推理过程转向被篡改的计算路径，利用原始路径与反转路径之间的严重不一致性来瓦解模型的表征能力。

Result: 在最新的自适应跟踪器上进行了大量实验，结果表明API框架在扰动隐蔽性、攻击有效性和推理速度三个方面均优于现有方法，实现了更隐蔽的对抗攻击、更强的攻击效果和更快的攻击推理速度。

Conclusion: 该工作为动态跟踪网络的安全性分析开辟了新的维度，并为未来构建鲁棒的自适应跟踪架构提供了重要的理论警示。

Abstract: Resource constraints on UAV platforms have driven a paradigm shift in aerial tracking, from pursuing performance toward balancing accuracy with efficiency. Adaptive Transformer Trackers, which leverage an input-dependent dynamic routing architecture, have emerged as a representative solution to this challenge. However, we reveal that behind this computation-on-demand flexibility hides a critical structural flaw: the Lipschitz singularity of computational path decisions, which has an unbounded local Lipschitz constant at discrete layer-skipping decision boundaries. This mathematical discontinuity renders adaptive tracking networks inherently unstable: tiny input perturbations can be amplified at the gating modules, causing dramatic changes in the inference topology. We formally characterize this singularity in the context of adaptive tracking architectures and, for the first time, identify it as a directly exploitable new attack surface. This insight reveals a previously overlooked and highly vulnerable topological path space attack surface. Based on this, we propose the Adversarial Path-Inversion (API) framework. API generates imperceptible perturbations to precisely manipulate the gating decisions, forcing the inference onto altered computational paths. The severe inconsistency between the original and the inverted paths dismantles the representation capability of the model. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art adaptive trackers demonstrate that API achieves superior perturbation stealthiness, more effective attack, and faster inference speeds. This work opens a new dimension for the security analysis of dynamic tracking networks and provides a theoretical warning for constructing robust adaptive tracking architectures in the future.

</details>


### [311] [Socially Grounded Agentic AI: Coordinating Plural Perspectives through Social Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03910)
*Matt Ratto,Abhishek Moturu,Daniel Silver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将多元化对齐（pluralistic alignment）从"输出多样化"重新定位为"社会性协调"问题，借鉴社会学理论（角色、互动、权力与专业领域）为AI系统设计提供概念与设计资源，包括基于角色的表征、视角间的结构化协调及情境敏感评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在多样化社会情境中部署，单一价值观的优化框架已不再适用。虽然多元化对齐受到关注，但现有方法缺乏对价值观如何在实践中被社会组织、竞争与协调的清晰理解。因此需要引入社会理论来弥补这一概念空白。

Method: 论文借鉴社会学既有传统，论证视角如何被角色结构化、通过互动塑造、并分布在权力与专业领域中。将这些洞见转化为AI设计的具体含义，包括基于角色的表征、视角间的结构化协调机制、以及情境敏感评估；对智能体系统而言，需要对齐的不只是最终输出，还包括角色激活、审议轨迹、聚合规则与反馈回路。

Result: 论文贡献在于将多元化对齐重新定位为社会性协调问题而非输出多样化问题，勾勒出一个让系统以结构化、可问责方式参与多视角交互的设计空间。

Conclusion: 作者确定了未来在真实世界环境中实现和实证评估这些方法的若干研究方向。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse social contexts, alignment can no longer be framed as the optimization of a single, unified set of values. Instead, systems must be able to recognize, represent, and respond to multiple legitimate perspectives. This has led to growing interest in pluralistic alignment, which seeks to move beyond one-size-fits-all models of appropriate behaviour. However, current approaches often lack a clear account of how values are socially organized, contested, and coordinated in practice. In this paper, we argue that social theory provides essential conceptual and design resources for addressing these challenges. Drawing on established traditions in sociology, we show how perspectives can be understood as structured by roles, shaped through interaction, and distributed across fields of power and expertise. We translate these insights into concrete implications for AI system design, including role-based representations, structured coordination among perspectives, and context-sensitive evaluation. For agentic systems, this requires aligning not only final outputs, but also the role activations, deliberative traces, aggregation rules, and feedback loops through which those outputs are produced. Our contribution is to reposition pluralistic alignment as a problem of socially grounded coordination rather than output diversification. We outline a design space for systems that engage multiple perspectives in structured and accountable ways, and we identify directions for future work to implement and empirically evaluate these approaches in real-world settings.

</details>


### [312] [Implementing Causal Perception: Competing SCMs and Situated Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03917)
*Jose M. Álvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了Álvarez和Ruggieri (2025)提出的因果感知（Causal Perception）框架。该框架描述的是拥有不同结构因果模型（SCM）的智能体在相同干预下推断出不同概率分布的现象。作者将结构性和参数性因果感知操作化，设计了计算干预分布和反事实分布的算法，并提出距离度量来量化智能体之间的分歧。通过在German Credit数据集上的实验，展示了因果感知如何影响多专家决策场景中的准确性和公平性，以及感知判决对距离度量和阈值的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果感知框架虽然提供了研究不同智能体对同一系统认知差异的富有前景的概率框架，但此前仅停留在纯理论层面，缺乏实际实现。作者旨在填补这一空白，将理论转化为可操作的算法和工具，并实证检验因果感知对公平性评估和决策的实际影响。

Method: 1) 将因果感知操作化为两种类型：结构性因果感知（智能体对因果图结构存在分歧）和参数性因果感知（智能体对图结构一致但对权重有分歧）；2) 设计用于计算干预分布和反事实分布的算法；3) 提出合适的距离度量来量化智能体间的分歧；4) 使用German Credit数据集在多专家决策场景中实验验证。

Result: 1) 因果感知影响多专家决策中的准确性和公平性；2) 感知判决对距离度量的选择和阈值设定敏感；3) 因果感知会改变公平性评估和基于阈值的决策；4) 偏见相对于智能体的SCM具有情境性（situated），表明公平性问题中各智能体竞争性世界观不可忽视。

Conclusion: 因果感知不仅是理论概念，而且具有实际影响：它改变了公平性评估和决策结果。偏见相对于智能体的因果模型是情境化的，这意味着在公平性问题中不能忽略不同智能体持有的竞争性世界观。

Abstract: Causal perception occurs when agents with competing Structural Causal Models (SCMs) of the same system infer different probability distributions, including the hypothetical distributions implied by each agent's SCM under the same set of interventions. It shapes how agents reason about the system and how they perceive its fairness. Causal perception is a promising probabilistic framework, but it has remained purely theoretical. This work provides the first implementation of the causal perception framework of Álvarez and Ruggieri (2025). We operationalize structural (agents disagree on the causal graph) and parametrical (agents agree on the causal graph but disagree on its weights) causal perception. We design algorithms for computing interventional and counterfactual distributions and propose suitable distance measures to quantify the disagreement. Using the German Credit dataset, we illustrate how causal perception affects accuracy and fairness in a multi-expert decision setting. We show that the perception verdict is sensitive to the choice of distance metric and threshold. We also show that causal perception changes fairness assessments and threshold-based decisions. Bias proves situated with respect to the agent's SCM, demonstrating that competing worldviews in fairness problems cannot be ignored.

</details>


### [313] [The Transformer Revolution, Part 1: Dynamic Processing through Output- Weight Interconnections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03921)
*Marco Giunti,Fabrizia Giulia Garavaglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对Transformer推理过程的新的解释视角，认为Transformer构建并应用由提示（prompt）动态生成的参数化变换，而非仅仅是"随机鹦鹉"式的统计复制。作者将这种计算形式命名为SIDPP（序列级交互式动态并行处理），并论证其可能在人脑中也有相应的神经实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望反驳"随机鹦鹉"观点——即大语言模型只是复现训练中学到的统计规律。他们认为需要一种更深刻、更具解释力的框架来理解Transformer在推理时的实际计算机制，从而推动可解释性、可控性和更小规模系统设计的发展。

Method: 提出了SIDPP框架，将Transformer解释为"用概念变换概念"的系统：token向量是被变换的概念，由矩阵和向量定义的参数化变换是变换性的概念。这些变换可以是静态的（训练时固定）或动态的（从输入序列生成）。机械上对应于简单的神经网络组，其架构新颖之处在于输出-权重互联（output-weight interconnections）与普通的输出-输入互联并存，使得一些网络的输出决定另一些网络的权重。

Result: 论文指出动态处理的贡献随提示长度增长，可能等于甚至超过静态处理，称之为"强提示敏感性"（strong prompt sensitivity）。此外，作者论证了人脑神经系统具备实现SIDPP所需的机制，因此推测人脑语言处理本身可能也是SIDPP的一种形式。

Conclusion: SIDPP框架对Transformer的可解释性、可预测性、可控性以及设计更小更可持续的AI系统都有重要意义。更进一步，该框架搭建了Transformer与人类神经认知之间的桥梁，提出人类语言处理与Transformer在功能架构上可能存在深层相似性。

Abstract: This paper offers a new interpretation of the Transformer during inference. Against the "stochastic parrot" view that large language models merely reproduce statistical regularities learned in training, we argue that Transformers construct and apply prompt-dependent transformations whose parameters are generated during inference. We call this form of computation SIDPP: Sequence-level Interactive Dynamic Parallel Processing. The Transformer is interpreted as a system that transforms concepts by means of concepts. Token vectors are the concepts to be transformed; parameterized transformations defined by matrices and vectors are the transforming concepts. These may be static, when fixed through training, or dynamic, when generated from the input sequence. Mechanically, they correspond to groups of simple neural networks. The Transformer's architectural novelty lies in output-weight interconnections, through which the outputs of some networks determine the weights of others, alongside ordinary output-input interconnections. By means of these interconnections, the system constructs transformations from the prompt and uses them to modify token representations. The contribution of dynamic processing grows with prompt length and may equal or exceed that of static processing, a phenomenon we call strong prompt sensitivity. This account bears on interpretability, predictability, control, and the design of smaller, more sustainable systems. Finally, since the human neural system possesses the mechanisms required to implement SIDPP, we argue that a form of SIDPP may, in principle, be neurally realized in the cerebral cortex. We therefore conjecture that human language processing may itself be a form of SIDPP produced by a functional architecture relevantly similar to that of the Transformer.

</details>


### [314] [TACT: Taxonomy-Aligned Post-Training for Pedagogically Adaptive English Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03952)
*Dongjie Yang,Siyan Lin,Leixian Shen,Rui Sheng,Huamin Qu,Zixin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TACT框架，通过两套互补分类法（导师策略分类法和学生行为分类法）构建TACTCorpus语料库和TACTBench基准，并后训练Qwen3.5-4B模型得到TACTutor，在脚手架质量、基准测试和人工评估中均优于基线模型和商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽广泛用于ESL对话练习，但有效教学需要基于学习者行为和对话上下文选择适当的教学策略，而现有LLM缺乏对教育文献中自适应教学原则的系统整合。

Method: 基于文献构建两套分类法（13种导师响应策略的Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy和按动作类型与状态分类的Student-Move Taxonomy）；构建包含260段真实师生对话、32,379条标注的TACTCorpus；通过监督微调和分类法对齐的Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）对Qwen3.5-4B进行后训练，优化脚手架质量而非仅模仿参考回答。

Result: 在TACTBench（78个真实教学情境）上，TACTutor相比骨干模型提升20.30%，超越所有评估的商业基线模型；在外部教育基准上保持骨干模型性能；50名学习者的盲测中获最高总体平均评分。

Conclusion: TACT为开发教学适应性ESL导师提供了开放的基础（数据、基准、模型权重），证明将教育学分类法整合到LLM训练中可显著提升教学互动质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to provide conversational practice for English-as-a-second-language (ESL) learners. Effective ESL tutoring, however, requires more than fluent response generation: a tutor must select an appropriate pedagogical action based on learner behavior and dialogue context. Human-tutoring research offers principles for adaptive support, but they are often task-specific and remain insufficiently integrated into LLM-based ESL tutor training and evaluation. We present TACT (Taxonomy-Aligned Conversational Tutor), a human-grounded framework for post-training and evaluating pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors. Drawing on established literature, we develop two complementary taxonomies: the Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy with 13 tutor response strategies and the Student-Move Taxonomy characterizing learner behavior by move type and status. Using these taxonomies, we construct TACTCorpus, which enriches 260 authentic teacher-student conversations with 32,379 annotations and quality-controlled augmented training data. We then post-train Qwen3.5-4B through supervised fine-tuning followed by taxonomy-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization, producing TACTutor and optimizing it for scaffolding quality rather than reference imitation alone. On TACTBench, a strategy-balanced diagnostic benchmark comprising 78 authentic tutoring contexts, TACTutor improves over its backbone by 20.30% and outperforms all evaluated proprietary baselines under the same protocol, while maintaining backbone performance on established external educational benchmarks; in a blinded study with 50 learners, it also receives the highest overall mean rating among the evaluated tutors. We release the data, benchmark, and model weights, providing an open foundation for developing pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors.

</details>


### [315] [A game theory for foundation models shows new paths to rational cooperation through similarity inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03958)
*Alexander Meulemans,Maciej Wołczyk,Marissa A. Weis,Rajai Nasser,Roberta Rocca,Seijin Kobayashi,Guillaume Lajoie,Angelika Steger,Blake Richards,Marcus Hutter,James Manyika,Rif A. Saurous,João Sacramento,Blaise Agüera y Arcas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现基础模型智能体在经典社会困境中通过最优规划会稳定收敛到合作，这直接违背了经典博弈论中相互背叛的预测。作者提出了"嵌入式贝叶斯智能体"理论模型和"嵌入式均衡"解概念来解释这一现象，为现代AI智能体的社会行为提供了新的博弈论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基础模型驱动的自主智能体被整合到社会和经济系统中，理解其集体行为原则对确保安全和合作至关重要。经典博弈论建立在"解耦能动性"假设之上（智能体将自身决策视为独立于环境和他人），但现代AI智能体是联合预测自身未来行动和外部观测的，因此经典框架无法解释和预测其社会行为。

Method: 作者提出"嵌入式贝叶斯智能体"理论模型，将智能体从解耦能动性转向嵌入式能动性，即智能体将自己建模为所居住宇宙的一部分，对自身决策算法保持认知不确定性。核心机制是相似性推断：通过推断他人是否与自己行为相似，嵌入式智能体将自身规划过程中的思考视为证据——决定合作就预示着相似伙伴也会做出类似决定。作者以"嵌入式均衡"取代纳什均衡作为新的解概念。

Result: 实验显示，在风格化的社会困境中，基础模型智能体进行最优规划时一致地收敛到稳定合作，直接与经典博弈论预测（相互背叛）相矛盾。理论分析表明嵌入式均衡能够形式化解释这一合作涌现现象。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了基础模型智能体在社会互动中的"嵌入式能动性"是合作涌现的关键机制，并为现代AI智能体的社会行为提供了超越经典博弈论的新理论基础，对AI安全和多智能体系统设计具有深远意义。

Abstract: As autonomous agents powered by foundation models are increasingly integrated into social and economic systems, understanding the principles governing their collective behavior is essential for ensuring safety and cooperation. Classical game theory, the dominant framework for modeling rational interaction, is built upon the assumption of `decoupled agency,' where agents treat their own decision-making as independent of the environment and other actors. Modern AI agents, however, jointly predict their own future actions alongside external observations. Here, we report a striking finding: when interacting in stylized social dilemmas, foundation model agents engaging in optimal planning consistently converge to stable cooperation, directly contradicting classical game-theoretic predictions of mutual defection. To understand this phenomenon, we introduce the `embedded Bayesian agent,' a theoretical model for foundation model agents. By shifting from decoupled to embedded agency, these agents model themselves as part of the universe they inhabit, maintaining epistemic uncertainty about their own decision-making algorithms. We show that by inferring whether others are behaviorally similar, an embedded agent treats its own deliberation during planning as evidence: a decision to cooperate predicts a similar decision by a similar partner. We formalize this mechanism of similarity inference through the `embedded equilibrium,' a novel solution concept replacing the Nash equilibrium to provide a foundational game theory for the social behavior of modern AI agents.

</details>


### [316] [Interpretable Adaptive Sampling for LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03961)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应的测试时缩放（test-time scaling）方法，使用轻量级模糊控制器根据提示复杂度与模型置信度等可解释信号，为每个查询动态分配采样预算，在减少平均采样数的同时保持与全预算控制相近的性能，使得推理计算量可检查而非固定或不可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时缩放方法通常使用固定的逐查询预算，对简单和困难提示花费相同计算量，效率低下；且固定预算缺乏可解释性，无法解释为何特定提示获得特定数量的采样。

Method: 提出自适应测试时缩放框架，核心是一个轻量级模糊控制器，它将估计的提示复杂度与模型置信度等可解释信号映射到每个查询的采样预算。在公平对齐协议下（匹配解码设置、受控答案选择）评估，与best-of-N、计算感知缩放、基于自置信度的基线进行比较。

Result: 在问答和数学推理任务上的多个模型与数据集中，自适应模糊控制在减少平均采样数的同时，优于多个标准基线，且与选择器匹配的全预算控制性能接近。

Conclusion: 可解释的自适应采样是大语言模型更高效测试时推理的一个实用方向。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves LLM reasoning by generating and aggregating multiple candidate answers, yet many pipelines use fixed per-query budgets that spend the same compute on easy and difficult prompts. These fixed budgets are also difficult to inspect because they do not explain why a given prompt receives a particular number of samples. We propose adaptive} test-time scaling with a lightweight fuzzy controller that maps interpretable signals, including estimated prompt complexity and model confidence, to a per-query sampling budget. The controller assigns fewer samples to easier or more confident prompts and more samples to harder or less certain prompts, making inference-time compute inspectable rather than fixed or opaque. We evaluate under a fair-alignment protocol with matched decoding settings and controlled answer selection, and compare against best-of-$N$, compute-aware scaling, and self-certainty-based baselines on question-answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across models and datasets, adaptive fuzzy control improves over several standard baselines and remains close to a selector-matched full-budget control while reducing the average number of samples. These findings suggest that interpretable adaptive sampling is a practical direction for more efficient test-time reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [317] [Should We Type or Talk to LLM Agents? A Comprehensive Study of Voice and Keyboard Input Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03970)
*Zizhao Hu,Nathan Elijah Segura,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HIVE（人类输入变异引擎），系统评估了语音转录扰动（如口误、停顿、转写结构）和QWERTY键盘扰动（拼写错误）对大型语言模型性能的影响，并通过七项研究发现揭示了两类扰动对模型鲁棒性的不同影响机制及恢复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过打字或语音两种方式向语言模型输入信息，每条通道都会留下独特的特征：键盘输入带有拼写噪声，语音输入则带有转写带来的口误（disfluency）和AI辅助听写工具引入的重构。这些自然发生的人类输入变异如何影响LLM的性能是一个尚未被系统研究的问题。

Method: 作者构建了HIVE（Human Input-Variation Engine）工具套件，包含两类扰动：（1）语音转录扰动（模拟转写中的口误和结构变化）；（2）QWERTY键盘扰动（模拟打字拼写错误）。利用HIVE在指令微调模型上进行系统评估，并通过实验设计区分不同因素（如转写结构vs填充词、token保留率、多选vs推理任务、测试集污染、轻量适配、思考预算等）对模型性能的影响。

Result: 七项核心发现：（i）语音转录扰动在每一类指令微调模型上都降低准确率，且成本来自转写的结构而非填充词；（ii）键盘扰动成本更低，模型能吸收大量拼写错误；（iii）两类扰动都归因于问题token的存活率：破坏token才造成伤害，新增token代价很小；（iv）两类通道的差距仅出现在需要构建或推导答案的任务上，多选任务无差异；（v）危害并非仅来自测试集污染；（vi）轻量适配无法消除此问题；（vii）思考预算几乎能完全恢复键盘通道的性能，但对口语化表达无效，且对压缩语音甚至更差。

Conclusion: 人类输入变异对LLM性能有系统性影响，语音通道的伤害大于键盘通道，且伤害机制根源于token破坏而非token增加。虽然思考预算能恢复键盘通道，但语音通道的鲁棒性问题需要更根本的解决方案。

Abstract: Human input reaches language models by typing or speaking, and each channel leaves a distinct signature: orthographic noise for keyboards; for voice, disfluency from conventional transcription and restructuring from AI-backed dictation tools. How do they impact an LLM's performance? In this paper we present HIVE (Human Input-Variation Engine), a suite of voice transcription perturbations and QWERTY keyboard perturbations. We use HIVE to evaluate how robust models are to these perturbations. We present seven findings. (i) Voice transcription perturbations lower accuracy across every instruction-tuned model we test, and it is the structure of the transcription rather than its fillers that carries the cost. (ii) QWERTY keyboard perturbations cost less, and a model absorbs a lot of them before accuracy falls away. (iii) Both trace back to one cause, how many of the question's tokens survive the perturbation: destroying a token is what hurts, while adding new ones alongside it costs little. (iv) The gap between the two channels appears only where the answer must be constructed or deduced; on multiple choice there is none. (v) The harm does not solely come from test-set contamination. (vi) It cannot be trained away with lightweight adaptation. (vii) A thinking budget recovers the keyboard channel almost entirely but leaves the spoken registers untouched, and compressed speech is worse with it.

</details>


### [318] [ReflectRL: Learning from Golden Negative Trajectories via Reflective-to-Direct Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03972)
*Jinhe Bi,Chennan Zhou,Zengjie Jin,Aniri,Shuo Lu,Wenke Huang,Hu Cao,Xun Xiao,Zhihong Zhu,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReflectRL框架，利用专家模型在困难问题上失败的轨迹（称为Golden Negative Trajectories）作为反思信号而非直接模仿的示范，通过先进行反思推理再将行为迁移回直接推理的方式，在on-policy训练中提升大语言模型的推理能力。实验覆盖9个基准、4个LLM骨干和4种训练方法，均显示稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轨迹引导式on-policy训练方法依赖专家模型的黄金轨迹作为监督信号，但当专家在更难的问题上失败时，这些失败轨迹通常被直接丢弃为负样本，导致训练失去主要的监督来源。作者认为这些失败轨迹虽然不能作为示范来模仿，但可以作为有缺陷的轨迹来进行反思，从而挖掘出有价值的推理信号。

Method: 提出了ReflectRL框架，这是一个轻量级的即插即用框架，包含两个关键阶段：(1) 利用Golden Negative Trajectories引发生成式反思推理（Reflective Reasoning）；(2) 通过Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition将习得的推理行为迁移回直接推理（Direct Reasoning）模式，从而在不改变推理范式的前提下利用失败轨迹中的信息。

Result: 在9个基准测试、4种LLM骨干模型和4种on-policy训练方法上进行实验，ReflectRL一致地提升了推理性能，且引入的开销极小。实验还验证了"反思优势"（Reflection Advantage）的存在：对于困难问题，反思一条有缺陷的轨迹比从头直接求解更容易且更有效。

Conclusion: 失败轨迹不应被简单地丢弃，而可以被转化为有价值的反思信号来增强模型推理能力。ReflectRL通过"先反思后迁移"的策略，在不增加推理时开销的情况下显著提升推理性能，为on-policy训练中负样本的利用提供了新的范式。

Abstract: On-policy training has emerged as a powerful post-training paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, and is often enhanced by golden trajectories from stronger expert models. However, when the expert fails on harder problems, existing trajectory-guided methods lose their main source of supervision, and these failed trajectories are typically discarded as negative samples. We argue that such failures, which we call Golden Negative Trajectories, can still provide valuable reasoning signals when treated not as demonstrations to imitate, but as flawed trajectories to reflect upon. We identify a Reflection Advantage: for hard problems, reflecting on a flawed trajectory can be easier and more effective than solving the problem directly from scratch. Motivated by this, we propose ReflectRL, a lightweight plug-and-play framework that learns from Golden Negative Trajectories during on-policy training. ReflectRL first uses these trajectories to elicit Reflective Reasoning, then applies Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition to transfer the acquired reasoning behavior back to Direct Reasoning. Experiments across 9 benchmarks, 4 LLM backbones, and 4 on-policy training methods show that ReflectRL consistently improves reasoning performance with minimal overhead.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [319] [Coordinated Primary Frequency Regulation and Grid-Forming Control for Wind Turbine Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02813)
*Meng Chen,Yufei Xi,Lin Cheng,Florian Döfler,Ioannis Lestas*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对风电机组(WTG)在电网成型(GFM)控制下与下垂式一次调频控制存在不匹配的问题，提出一种WTG一次调频与GFM控制的协调策略，通过建立功率跟踪系数、功率设定点与频率偏差之间的设计关系，使GFM-WTGs在预设频率限值内一致地参与一次调频，同时保持适当功率点和有效利用允许的功率备用，且不牺牲传统GFM控制的控制结构和动态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GFM控制策略通常将直流源视为无约束环节，这在实际应用于风电机组时会产生不匹配问题，尤其是在一次调频场景下，GFM-WTGs的行为与基于下垂的一次调频控制之间存在系统性失配，需要解决这一矛盾。

Method: 提出一种WTG一次调频与GFM控制之间的新颖协调策略，通过建立功率跟踪系数、功率设定点与频率偏差之间的精心设计关系，使GFM-WTGs在预设频率界限内一致参与一次调频，同时保持合适的功率工作点并有效利用允许的功率备用容量。该方法保留了传统GFM控制的结构和动态性能，并能自然地适应不同风速条件。

Result: 在不同运行场景下的对比案例研究表明，所提策略的有效性和优越性得到了验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的协调策略能有效解决GFM-WTGs与下垂式一次调频之间的不匹配问题，在保持传统GFM控制结构和动态性能的同时，实现一次调频的协调参与并适应多变风速条件。

Abstract: Conventional grid-forming (GFM) control strategies often treat the DC source as an unconstrained link, creating mismatches when applied to the wind turbine generators (WTGs). Focusing on primary frequency regulation, this paper systematically investigates the mismatch between the GFM-WTGs behavior and the droop-based primary frequency regulation. To address this issue, a novel coordination strategy between WTG primary frequency regulation and GFM control is proposed. By establishing well-designed relationships among the power-tracking coefficient, power set-point, and frequency deviation, the proposed strategy enables GFM-WTGs to participate consistently in primary frequency regulation within predefined frequency limits while maintaining appropriate power points and effectively utilizing the allowable power reserve. Furthermore, the proposed method preserves the control structure and dynamic performance of conventional GFM control and inherently adapts to varying wind-speed conditions. Comparative case studies under different operating scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of the proposed strategy.

</details>


### [320] [Stabilization of First-Order Partial Integro-Differential Equations with Concurrent Input and State Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02851)
*Sanguan Zhong,Jie Qi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了一阶双曲型偏积分微分方程（PIDE）在同时存在输入时滞和状态时滞情况下的边界镇定问题，提出了一种基于反步法的边界控制器来实现镇定与时滞补偿。设计依赖于包含Fredholm型和Volterra型积分项的两个仿射Volterra变换，最终得到四组PIDE核方程。通过沿特征线划分核域为有限个三角子区域，并从前一子区域到下一子区域依次构造核解来建立良定性，同时证明了闭环系统的有限时间稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入时滞和状态时滞在同一系统中的共存使控制设计变得复杂，特别是在大输入时滞情况下需要预测更多状态信息，这给一阶双曲型PIDE的边界镇定带来了挑战，需要一个同时补偿两类时滞的系统性控制方法。

Method: 采用基于反步法（backstepping）的边界控制器设计。核心方法包括：(1) 构造两个包含Fredholm型和Volterra型积分项的仿射Volterra变换；(2) 由此推导出四组PIDE核方程；(3) 通过沿特征线将核域划分为有限个三角子区域，在相邻子区域间依次构造核解以建立方程组的良定性。

Result: 证明了所得闭环系统的有限时间稳定性，并通过数值仿真验证了所提出控制器的有效性。

Conclusion: 该论文为同时存在输入和状态时滞的一阶双曲型PIDE提供了有效的边界镇定方案，通过反步法与特征线分区方法解决了核方程良定性的难题，实现了有限时间镇定。

Abstract: This paper considers boundary stabilization problems for a first-order hyperbolic partial integro-differential equation (PIDE) subject to concurrent input and state delays. The coexistence of these two types of delays complicates control design, especially under the case of large input delay that requires to predict more state information. A backstepping-based boundary controller is developed to achieve stabilization and delay compensation. The design relies on two affine Volterra transformations involving both Fredholm- and Volterra-type integral terms, which results in a four-PIDE kernel equations. To establish their well-posedness, the kernel domain is partitioned along characteristic lines into a finite number of triangular subregions, and the kernel solution is constructed successively in the preceding subregion to the next one. The finite-time stability of the resulting closed-loop system is established. Numerical simulations are provided to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed controller.

</details>


### [321] [Sequential Operational Decision-Making for Power System Resilience Under Evolving Wildfires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02976)
*Arastoo H Salimi,Majid Dehghani,Hamidreza Nazaripouya*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型自动化决策支持框架，通过随机多阶段规划来增强电力系统在渐进式野火事件中的运行韧性。该框架同时考虑预防性和纠正性措施，基于野火演变过程中的潜在情景实现自动化自适应决策，并使用随机对偶动态规划算法求解全局最优解，在IEEE 30节点和300节点系统上验证了其有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火对电力系统的威胁是渐进演变的，传统单阶段运营优化策略无法适应这种动态变化的威胁。现有方法缺乏能够同时考虑野火演化过程中预防性与纠正性措施、并随时间自适应调整决策的框架，因此需要一种能够应对野火渐进式威胁的自动化决策支持方法。

Method: 将决策过程建模为野火渐进过程中的随机多阶段规划问题；提出一种新颖算法，基于野火进程和系统地理信息构建决策树；采用随机对偶动态规划（Stochastic Dual Dynamic Programming）方法求解提出的优化问题，实现框架的全局最优解。

Result: 在IEEE 30节点系统上验证了该方法在不同野火影响情景下的有效性，并在IEEE 300节点系统上展示了可扩展性。结果表明，所提出的自动化框架相比单阶段运营优化策略在增强电力系统韧性方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 该框架通过融合野火渐进演化的时空信息与多阶段随机优化，实现了对野火威胁的动态自适应响应，能够有效平衡野火风险、运营成本与负荷削减之间的关系，显著提升电力系统在野火条件下的整体韧性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel automated decision-support framework aimed at enhancing the resilience of power systems and operational resilience against wildfires by formulating the decision-making process as a stochastic multi-stage programming during a progressive wildfire. The paper develops a framework that takes into account both preventive and corrective actions, enabling automated and adaptive decisions based on potential scenarios over the course of a wildfire's progression. This approach considers the evolving nature of the wildfire threat and seeks to optimize the response strategies accordingly throughout its duration. The objective is to minimize wildfire risk and operational costs while reducing load curtailment. The framework accounts for potential contingencies caused by progressive wildfires. A novel algorithm is proposed to construct a decision tree based on wildfire progression and system geographical information. Additionally, a novel stochastic dual dynamic programming approach is deployed to solve the proposed optimization problem, achieving a global optimum for the framework. The effectiveness of the proposed method is demonstrated on the IEEE 30-bus system under various wildfire impact scenarios and is then applied to the IEEE 300-bus system to illustrate the scalability of the proposed approach. The results highlight the advantages of the proposed automated framework over a single-stage operational optimization strategy in enhancing power grid resilience under wildfire conditions.

</details>


### [322] [Enhancing Operational Grid Resilience Against Wildfires Under Decision-Dependent Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02978)
*Arastoo H Salimi,Hamidreza Nazaripouya*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的自动化决策框架，通过考虑决策依赖不确定性（DDU）来增强电力系统应对野火的韧性，融合预防性和纠正性措施，实现面对野火演变的自适应自动化决策，并在IEEE 30节点系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火对电力系统构成严重威胁，现有方法未充分考虑决策对不确定性情景的影响（即决策依赖不确定性），导致运营策略不够现实和具有韧性。需要一种能够自适应响应野火演变的自动化决策框架。

Method: 1. 建立基线多阶段优化模型作为基础；2. 在该模型中融入DDU，使前期阶段的公共安全停电（PSPS）决策影响未来野火情景的概率和参数；3. 采用数学分解算法高效求解复杂的优化问题。

Result: 在IEEE 30节点系统上的案例研究表明，将DDU的影响纳入优化过程后，所得方案在面对野火威胁时更加现实且在运营上更具韧性。

Conclusion: 考虑决策依赖不确定性的自动化决策框架能够有效提升电力系统在野火环境下的运营韧性，为野火驱动的电力系统调度提供了更可靠和自适应的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new automated decision-making framework to enhance the resilience of electrical systems against wildfires by applying operational strategies that account for decision-dependent uncertainty (DDU). The proposed framework incorporates both preventive and corrective measures, enabling adaptive and automated decision-making throughout the course of evolving wildfire scenarios. First, a baseline multistage optimization model is presented as a foundation to support wildfire-driven operational decision-making. The model then incorporates DDU, wherein Public Safety Power Shutoff (PSPS) decisions made in earlier stages influence the probabilities and parameters of future wildfire scenarios. To efficiently solve the resulting complex optimization problem, a mathematical decomposition algorithm is employed. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through case studies on the IEEE 30-bus system. Simulation results confirm that incorporating the impact of DDU into the optimization process provides more realistic, and operationally resilient solutions in the face of evolving wildfire threats.

</details>


### [323] [Process-Knowledge-Embedded Safe DRL for Real-Time Dispatch of Process Loads in Industrial Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03149)
*Daniyaer Paizulamu,Lin Cheng,Fashun Shi,Yuchi Zhang,Zhaoyang Dong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种嵌入工艺知识的安全深度强化学习框架，用于工业微电网中炼钢过程负荷（SPL）的实时调度。通过无损主动前沿动作空间、工艺距离引导的动作处理机制、递归过程可行性保障以及结合修正预算和原始对偶更新的PPO算法，实现了零过程损耗，相比规则调度和滚动MILP分别降低49.2%和25.9%的购电成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 炼钢过程负荷（SPL）作为工业微电网中的灵活资源，可提升本地可再生能源利用率和降低购电成本。但由于多阶段强耦合特性，当前决策会影响后续可行性，传统深度强化学习难以在降低成本的同时保证整个生产过程中的过程可行性。

Method: 1) 构建无损主动前沿动作空间；2) 设计工艺距离引导的动作处理机制，根据工艺距离和actor的安全动作偏好重新分配被排除动作的概率；3) 建立递归过程可行性以保证可容许执行和可行延续；4) 通过修正预算和原始对偶更新将期望工艺修正距离纳入PPO，将工艺知识内化到原始策略中；5) 推导出界以量化原始策略对安全处理的依赖程度。

Result: 基于真实数据的案例研究表明，该方法实现零过程损耗，相比规则调度节省49.2%购电成本，相比滚动MILP节省25.9%，且计算时间在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: 提出的工艺知识嵌入式安全深度强化学习框架能够有效解决SPL多阶段耦合下的实时调度问题，在保证过程可行性的同时显著降低购电成本，为工业微电网中复杂柔性负荷的智能调度提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Steelmaking process loads (SPLs) are flexible resources that enhance local renewable-energy utilization and reduce electricity procurement costs in industrial microgrids. However, strong multistage coupling makes current decisions affect subsequent feasibility, challenging conventional deep reinforcement learning to reduce costs while maintaining process feasibility throughout production. This paper proposes a process-knowledge-embedded safe deep reinforcement learning framework for the real-time dispatch of SPLs in industrial microgrids. Specifically, a lossless active-frontier action space is constructed, and a process-distance-guided action-processing mechanism reallocates excluded-action probabilities according to process distance and the actor's safe-action preference. Recursive process feasibility is established to guarantee admissible execution and feasible continuation. Furthermore, the expected process-correction distance is incorporated into PPO through a correction budget and a primal-dual update to internalize process knowledge into the raw policy, while a derived bound quantifies the raw policy's dependence on safety processing. Case studies using real-world data demonstrate zero process losses, electricity-cost reductions of 49.2% and 25.9% relative to rule-based scheduling and rolling MILP, respectively, within an acceptable computation time.

</details>


### [324] [Calibration of a Macroscopic Coupled People-Epidemic Transport PDE Model via Density and Velocity Computation from Microscopic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03157)
*Nikoletta Hadjihabi,Nikolaos Bekiaris-Liberis*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从微观个体轨迹数据中推导并平滑宏观密度和速度的方法，应用于足球场内的人群。研究者基于易感者与感染者之间的关键接触时长来识别暴露个体，从而分别计算易感、感染和暴露人群的宏观密度。随后提出并采用有限体积法数值求解了一个人群流动-疫情传播耦合的偏微分方程模型，最后利用平滑后的密度对模型进行了校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型公共场所（如体育场）中，人群聚集可能加速传染病传播。现有的微观轨迹数据虽然能提供个体级位置信息，但缺乏将其转化为可用于宏观疫情建模的密度场的方法。作者希望通过微观到宏观的桥接，结合人群流动与疫情传播模型，为公共场所的疫情风险评估提供工具。

Method: 首先从个体微观轨迹数据中派生并平滑宏观密度和速度场；通过检测易感者与感染者之间关键接触的持续时间来识别暴露个体，进而分别计算易感（S）、感染（I）和暴露（E）人群的宏观密度；然后提出一个耦合人群流动与疫情传播的偏微分方程（PDE）模型，并采用有限体积法进行数值求解；最后使用平滑后的密度数据对模型参数进行校准。

Result: 论文展示了从真实足球场人群轨迹数据中成功提取宏观密度场的过程，并通过有限体积法数值求解了耦合的流动-传播PDE模型，同时利用实测平滑密度完成了模型校准。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了从微观人群轨迹到宏观传染病传播模型的完整分析链条，可用于体育场等大型公共场所的疫情传播风险预测与防控策略评估。

Abstract: We introduce an approach for derivation and smoothing of macroscopic densities and velocities from people trajectories, obtained from microscopic position data of individuals within a football stadium. We compute macroscopic densities specifically for susceptible, infected, and exposed individuals, via detection of exposed individuals based on the duration of critical contacts between susceptible and infected. We then present and numerically solve a crowd flow - epidemic spreading PDE model using a finite volume scheme. Finally, we calibrate the model using the smoothed densities computed.

</details>


### [325] [GriD-LMIA: A Gridding-Based Assembler for Solving Differentiable Parameter-Dependent Linear Matrix Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03175)
*Yicheng Xu,Faryar Jabbari*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GriD-LMIA，一个基于网格划分的MATLAB工具包，用于将连续域上依赖参数的线性矩阵不等式（PD-LMIs）转换为有限个充分线性矩阵不等式，便于半定规划求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PD-LMIs需要在连续参数域上成立，而半定规划求解器只能处理有限数量的约束。当调度参数随时间变化时，参数依赖决策变量的导数也会进入条件中，这增加了处理难度。因此需要一种方法将无限约束转化为有限约束。

Method: 该方法首先用超矩形网格对参数域进行划分，在每格上用张量Bernstein多项式表示已知数据和决策变量。然后通过YALMIP导出三种证书形式：直接Bernstein、Pólya提升（Pólya-elevated）和基于平方和（sum-of-squares）的充分条件，从而将连续域的PD-LMIs转化为有限个充分LMIs。

Result: 通过示例验证了该方法，考察了网格密度、决策变量阶数和证书选择之间的权衡关系，展示了不同配置对求解结果的影响。

Conclusion: GriD-LMIA为处理连续域上带导数约束的PD-LMIs提供了一种实用且灵活的MATLAB工具，支持多种证书形式以适应不同问题的需求。

Abstract: Parameter-dependent linear matrix inequalities (PD-LMIs) require holding over a continuous domain. When the scheduling parameters vary with time, derivatives of parameter-dependent decisions may also enter the conditions. Since semidefinite programming solvers require finitely many constraints, we introduce GriD-LMIA, the Gridding-based Differentiable PD-LMI Assembler. It converts the conditions that need to hold on a continuous domain into finitely many sufficient LMIs in MATLAB. It first partitions the domain with a hyper-rectangular grid and represents known data and decisions on each cell with tensor Bernstein polynomials. The package exports direct Bernstein, Pólya-elevated, and sum-of-squares-based certificates through YALMIP. Examples are given to examine the balance among grid density, decision degree, and certificate choice.

</details>


### [326] [Dynamic Flexibility Requests in Local Flexibility Markets: Quantifying the DSO Willingness to Pay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03226)
*Savvas Panagi,Chrysovalantis Spanias,Petros Aristidou*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动态灵活性请求方法，将DSO的支付意愿直接嵌入市场出清目标中，通过将变压器和电缆老化、网络损耗及电压拥塞货币化实现。采用精确凸分段线性epigraph重构IEEE C57.91变压器老化模型和基于Arrhenius的电缆老化模型，并证明其精确性。在改进的CIGRE中压基准网络上验证，相比现有方法显著提高了市场流动性、采购效率和网络运行水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LFM市场出清方法存在两大缺陷：一是依赖不切实际的集中式AC-OPF出清算法；二是灵活性请求高度局部化、静态化，主要由拥塞管理驱动，忽略了灵活性的经济价值。这导致DSO在确定灵活性采购量和支付意愿时缺乏合理依据。

Method: 1) 将变压器老化（IEEE C57.91模型）、电缆老化（Arrhenius模型）、网络损耗和电压拥塞均货币化，并将DSO支付意愿直接嵌入市场出清目标函数。2) 开发精确凸分段线性epigraph重构方法处理老化模型的非线性，并形式化证明保真性。3) 在改进的CIGRE中压基准网络上进行验证。

Result: 在改进的CIGRE中压基准网络上与最新方法对比，结果表明所提框架能显著提高市场流动性、实现更高效的灵活性采购，并改善网络运行状态，同时保持市场的透明性和非歧视性原则。

Conclusion: 该动态灵活性请求方法克服了现有方法在集中式出清和不合理灵活性请求方面的局限，通过将资产老化和网络经济性因素融入市场目标，实现了更高效、更具经济价值的灵活性市场设计。

Abstract: Local Flexibility Markets (LFMs) require Distribution System Operators (DSOs) to determine both the quantity of flexibility to procure and the corresponding willingness to pay during market clearing. Existing approaches typically rely on unrealistic centralized AC-OPF clearing algorithms or strictly localized, static flexibility requests driven primarily by congestion management, while the economic value of flexibility is largely neglected. This paper proposes a dynamic flexibility-request methodology in which the DSO's willingness to pay is embedded directly into the market-clearing objective by monetizing transformer and cable aging, network losses, and voltage congestion. To enable computationally efficient clearing, exact convex piecewise-linear epigraph reformulations of the IEEE C57.91 transformer aging model and an Arrhenius-based cable aging model are developed and formally proven to preserve exactness. The proposed framework is validated on a modified CIGRE MV benchmark and compared with a recent state-of-the-art flexibility-request methodology. The results demonstrate significantly higher market liquidity, more efficient flexibility procurement, and improved network operations while preserving the market's transparency and non-discrimination principles.

</details>


### [327] [Partitioned Mixed Small Gain-Phase Decentralized Stability Criterion for Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03641)
*Diego Cifelli,Adolfo Anta*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分区混合增益-相位分散式稳定性判据，允许异构电网中不同子集的变流器在同一频率下分别满足小增益或小相位条件，克服了现有分散式判据要求所有变流器满足相同类型条件的保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着变流器接口资源渗透率提高，包含构网型和跟网型变流器的异构电网稳定性评估变得困难。现有的分散式混合小增益和小相位判据虽然具有可扩展性，但要求所有变流器在同一频率下满足相同类型的条件，无法同时利用跟网型变流器的低增益特性和构网型变流器的有利相位特性，导致不必要的保守性。

Method: 提出分区混合增益-相位分散式稳定性判据：允许变流器子集在同一频率下分别满足不同的局部条件（一部分通过小增益界认证，另一部分通过小相位界认证）。增益与相位裕度之间的可容许权衡由网络相关的二次约束决定。论文刻画了可容许的增益和相位界集合，建立凸性和有界性性质，并开发了选择这些界的实用流程。

Result: 在包含构网型和跟网型变流器的异构系统上进行了验证，包括双变流器系统和IEEE 39节点系统，结果优于标准的分散式小增益和小相位条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的分区混合判据通过允许不同变流器子集采用各自有利的局部认证条件，减少了保守性，同时保持局部性和可扩展性，为异构电网稳定性评估提供了更灵活且技术感知的分散式方案。

Abstract: The increasing penetration of converter-interfaced resources is making power-system stability assessment more challenging, particularly in heterogeneous grids containing both grid-forming and grid-following converters. Existing decentralized mixed small-gain and small-phase criteria provide scalable stability certificates, but they require all converters to satisfy the same type of condition at a given frequency. As a result, they cannot simultaneously exploit the low gain of grid-following converters and the favorable phase properties of grid-forming converters, leading to unnecessary conservatism. This paper proposes a partitioned mixed gain-phase decentralized stability criterion that allows distinct subsets of converters to satisfy different local requirements at the same frequency. Specifically, one subset can be certified through small-gain bounds, while the complementary subset is certified through small-phase bounds. The admissible trade-off between gain and phase margins is determined by a network-dependent quadratic constraint, yielding a technology-aware stability certificate that remains local at the converter level. The paper characterizes the admissible set of gain and phase bounds, establishes useful convexity and boundedness properties, and develops a practical procedure for selecting these bounds. The proposed method is demonstrated on heterogeneous systems containing grid-forming and grid-following converters, including a two-converter system and the IEEE 39-bus system, outperforming the standard decentralized small-gain and small-phase conditions.

</details>


### [328] [Precision Specimen Positioning in Electron Microscopy through Hysteresis Compensation, Iterative Learning, and Vision-Based Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03669)
*J. S. van Hulst,A. M. C. de Peffer,D. Herceg,E. M. Franken,E. Verschueren,W. P. M. H. Heemels,D. J. Antunes*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套集成的前馈控制框架，用于在运行中的电子显微镜内实现高精度样品定位。该框架结合了逐元件迟滞补偿、基于EM图像的POI测量、编码器代理信号构建以及换向角域迭代学习控制，在实验室装置上减少POI跟踪误差13倍以上，在运行中的透射电子显微镜上减少7-12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子显微镜需要纳米级精度的长行程样品定位。压电步进驱动器适合此任务，但其精度受迟滞、机械偏差和非共位传感限制。先前工作仅在简化的实验室装置上解决这些问题，而将其扩展到完整电子显微镜载物台时面临耦合非线性运动学和缺乏专用POI传感器的挑战。

Method: 1) 逐元件迟滞补偿用于线性化执行器响应；2) 通过基于互相关的图像跟踪从EM图像构建POI测量；3) 基于该测量构建编码器代理信号用于POI位置估计；4) 采用换向角域迭代学习控制（ILC），以代理信号作为误差信号来消除步进中的可重复扰动，学习到的修正以换向角为参数，可跨准静态驱动频率范围迁移。

Result: 该框架在实验室装置上将POI跟踪误差减少13倍以上，在运行中的透射电子显微镜上减少7至12倍。

Conclusion: 该集成前馈框架成功解决了在没有专用POI传感器的运行中的电子显微镜载物台上的精密定位问题，通过多层次的补偿和学习控制策略显著提升了定位精度。

Abstract: Electron microscopy requires nanometer-scale specimen positioning over a long stroke. Piezo-stepper actuators are well suited for this task, but their accuracy is limited by hysteresis, mechanical misalignments, and non-collocated sensing. Prior work has addressed these limitations on simplified lab setups. However, extending to a full electron microscope stage introduces coupled nonlinear kinematics and, importantly, the absence of a dedicated point-of-interest (POI) sensor. This paper presents an integrated feedforward framework for precision positioning on such a stage inside an operational electron microscope. Per-element hysteresis compensation first linearizes the actuator response. In the absence of a dedicated POI sensor, a POI measurement is constructed from EM images through cross-correlation-based image tracking. From this measurement, we construct an encoder-based proxy for the POI position.
