Commutation-angle-domain iterative learning control then uses this proxy as its error signal to cancel the repeatable disturbances of stepping. Because the learned corrections are parameterized in the commutation angle, they transfer across the quasi-static range of drive frequencies. The framework reduces the POI tracking error by over 13x on the lab setup and by 7 to 12x on an operational transmission electron microscope.

</details>


### [329] [A Conductance Based Amygdala Model of Threat Processing in Anxiety and Depression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03712)
*Malik Faizan,P. J. White,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一个包含九方程的传导型计算模型，将杏仁核失调与心血管应激反应联系起来，用于数字表型分析和临床解释。模型在Hodgkin-Huxley框架基础上扩展了应对能力、感知应激负荷和前额叶调节强度三个临床调节因子，能够生成心率与血压轨迹，并在三个独立数据集中验证了与真实世界应激相关自主神经模式的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 焦虑和抑郁障碍被视为连续应激调节维度上的失调，但现有计算方法很少将可解释的回路机制与自主神经生理学联系起来，缺乏能桥接二者并提供临床可解释性的计算框架。

Method: 开发了一个紧凑的九方程基于电导的杏仁核-下丘脑-心血管通路模型。该框架扩展了Hodgkin-Huxley形式体系，引入三个临床调节因子（应对能力、感知应激负荷、前额叶调控强度），并整合了慢速历史依赖内部状态、自适应阈值、分级威胁识别和压力感受器耦合的下丘脑整合功能来生成心率与血压轨迹。

Result: 模型呈现出强、中、弱三种稳定调节状态作为单一闭环系统的稳定工作点。鲁棒性分析表明随机变异和参数扰动下状态分离仍保持，消融研究确定自适应阈值是驱动定量状态分离的主要机制。模拟的心血管反应在已知应激生理范围内，并在三个独立数据集中与真实世界应激相关自主神经模式高度吻合。

Conclusion: 一个紧凑的机制模型能够将心理测量调节因子、杏仁核兴奋性和下游心血管输出统一纳入单一可解释框架内，为机制驱动的数字表型分析、患者个体化应激监测及心理健康决策支持的未来数字孪生方法提供了计算基础。

Abstract: Anxiety and depressive disorders are increasingly viewed as dysregulations along continuous stress-regulatory dimensions. However, existing computational approaches seldom connect interpretable circuit level mechanisms to autonomic physiology. Methods: This study develops a mechanistic framework that links amygdala dysregulation to cardiovascular stress responses for digital phenotyping and clinical interpretation. We formulated a compact, nine equation, conductance based model of the amygdala hypothalamus cardiovascular pathway. The framework extends Hodgkin Huxley formalism with three clinically grounded modulators: coping capacity, perceived stress load, and prefrontal regulatory strength. A slow, history-dependent internal state, adaptive thresholding, graded threat acknowledgement, and baroreflex coupled hypothalamic integration were used to generate heart rate and blood pressure trajectories. Results: Distinct strong, moderate, and weak regulatory regimes emerged as stable operating states of a single closed-loop system. Robust analyses showed that stochastic variability and parameter perturbation preserved regime separation, while ablation studies identified the adaptive threshold as the principal mechanism driving quantitative regime separation. Simulated cardiovascular responses remained within reported stress physiology ranges. Furthermore, external evaluation across three independent datasets supported robust agreement with real world, stress related autonomic patterns. Conclusion: A compact, mechanistic model can jointly link psychometric modulators, amygdala excitability, and downstream cardiovascular output within a single, interpretable framework. Significance: This work provides a computationally tractable basis for mechanism informed digital phenotyping, patient specific stress monitoring, and future digital twin approaches for mental health decision support.

</details>


### [330] [Input-to-State Stability of Reset-Integral Sliding Mode Control for Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03802)
*Xinxin Zhang,Leonid Freidovich*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对集成单复位态复位控制器（RC）与积分滑模控制器（ISMC）的混合控制系统进行了稳定性分析，证明了滑动面可达性与名义复位机制解耦，并通过Lyapunov分析证明了闭环系统在外部参考和扰动下具有输入-状态稳定性（ISS）和一致最终有界性（UUB），在无扰动调节场景下恢复全局渐近稳定性（GAS），并通过数值案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复位控制器和积分滑模控制器各自具有优势，但二者结合的混合控制系统的稳定性尚未被严格分析。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，为该类混合控制系统的工程应用提供稳定性保障。

Method: 1) 将复位控制器（单复位态）与积分滑模控制器进行集成，构建混合控制系统模型；2) 证明滑动面的可达性与名义复位机制解耦；3) 利用该解耦特性，基于Lyapunov方法进行稳定性分析；4) 分别针对有外部参考/扰动和无扰动的调节场景进行稳定性论证。

Result: 1) 证明了滑动面可达性与复位机制的解耦性质；2) 闭环RC-ISMC系统在外部参考和扰动存在下满足ISS和UUB；3) 在无扰动调节场景下恢复GAS；4) 数值案例证实了理论结果的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文首次为RC-ISMC混合控制系统提供了严格的稳定性分析框架，确立了该系统的ISS、UUB和GAS性质，为该类控制器在实际工程中的可靠应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This work presents a stability analysis of a hybrid control system integrating a reset controller (RC) featuring a single reset state with an integral sliding-mode controller (ISMC). It is shown that the reachability of the sliding surface is decoupled from the nominal reset mechanism. This decoupling property enables a Lyapunov-based stability analysis, demonstrating that the closed-loop RC-ISMC system achieves input-to-state stability (ISS) and uniform ultimate boundedness (UUB) in the presence of exogenous references and disturbances. Furthermore, global asymptotic stability (GAS) is recovered in the unperturbed regulation scenario. A numerical case study illustrates the theoretical results.

</details>


### [331] [A Stackelberg-Bayesian Capacity-Market Game of Carbon Regulation and Second-Life Battery Investment under AI Data-Center Load Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03989)
*Rouzbeh Haghighi,Ali Hassan,Sina Mohammadi,Marcus Chen I Wada,Wencong Su*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一个三层Stackelberg-Bayesian博弈模型，分析AI数据中心带来的电力负荷增长对容量市场投资结构、碳排放和利润的影响，并评估碳税、可再生能源补贴及二次寿命电池（SLB）补贴等政策工具的成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心的快速扩张正导致美国各ISO/RTO区域电力负荷大幅增长，推高系统成本和碳排放。现有研究缺乏在容量市场背景下，将政策制定者、ISO和市场参与者之间的交互，以及技术不确定性和信息不完全性纳入统一框架的分析，因此需要一个新的博弈模型来系统评估政策干预的效果。

Method: 构建三层Stackelberg-Bayesian博弈模型：领导者为监管机构（设定碳惩罚与补贴），中间层为单一ISO容量市场（以经典发电扩展问题求解能量平衡），底层为技术特定的投资者（在信息不完全下决策容量与运行，形成贝叶斯纳什均衡）。AI负荷增长被简约地建模为绿地增量扩展中的额外负荷增长因子，并考虑二次寿命电池（SLB）与新型/首次寿命储能的竞争。

Result: 量化了碳税、可再生能源补贴和SLB补贴如何重塑均衡投资组合、碳排放和利润，并在成本效益和碳减排两个维度上比较了不同政策情景。

Conclusion: 该框架能够隔离AI负荷增长所引致的新增容量规模及其技术结构，为监管机构设计有效的碳定价与补贴政策以缓解AI数据中心扩张带来的碳排放压力提供决策参考。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) data centers are driving rapid electricity load growth across all U.S. ISO/RTO regions, raising both system costs and carbon exposure. This study develops a three-level Stackelberg--Bayesian game in which a regulator (leader) sets carbon penalties and subsidies, a single ISO capacity market clears against an energy balance modeled as a classical generation-expansion problem, and technology-specific investors (followers) decide capacity and operation under incomplete information, yielding a Bayesian Nash equilibrium. The AI impact is captured parsimoniously as an additional load-growth factor on a greenfield-incremental expansion, isolating how much new capacity the growth pulls in and which technology fills it. Within this framework, we consider second-life battery (SLB) storage competing against new/first-life storage for capacity-market revenue. We quantify how a carbon tax, a renewable subsidy, and an SLB subsidy reshape the equilibrium investment mix, carbon emissions, and profit. Different scenarios are compared at the end based on cost-effectiveness and reduced carbon emissions.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [332] [Bandwidth-Free Inference for Recursive Nonlinear Impulse Response Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02943)
*Guilherme Vianna*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性结构自回归模型中的递归脉冲响应估计问题，提出了基于经验分位数的半参数递归构造方法，建立了完整的\sqrt{T}渐近线性表示理论，并提供了无需带宽选择的可行推断方法（包括残差自助法和有限模拟路径的协方差修正）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的最接近半参数递归构造方法需要平滑估计创新分位数函数，但未发展直接经验残差实现的完整一阶推断理论。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为实际采用的直接经验残差方法提供严格的统计推断基础。

Method: 在有限维非线性结构自回归中，假设创新分布为无约束连续边际分布且冲击具有固定正态秩。估计器用生成的结构残差的经验分位数替代每个创新分位数函数，并迭代相同的结构转移。对任意固定响应集合，建立含四个分量的联合\sqrt{T}渐近线性表示：（1）直接转移估计；（2）转移估计对残差序统计量的影响；（3）普通创新分位数估计；（4）平移后的冲击分位数。通过递归投影后，分位数项具有残差秩与间距表示，无需创新密度估计或分位数平滑即可进行可行推断。此外还刻画了平滑的传播偏差，建立了完整递归残差自助法的有效性，并推导了有限模拟路径的额外协方差贡献。

Result: 建立了经验分位数递归脉冲响应估计器的联合\sqrt{T}渐近线性表示（四分量分解），获得了无需创新密度估计或分位数平滑的可行推断方法，证明了残差自助法的有效性和有限模拟路径的协方差修正，实现了对平滑递归构造中同一正态秩响应的无带宽经验残差推断。

Conclusion: 本文为非线性递归脉冲响应的经验残差实现提供了完备的一阶推断理论，解决了半参数递归构造中平滑方法缺乏直接经验实现推断的问题，并提供了带宽自由的推断框架。

Abstract: Recursive nonlinear impulse responses require an estimated innovation law whenever the impact shock is normalized by innovation ranks and future innovations are integrated out. The closest semiparametric recursive construction in the literature estimates the relevant innovation quantile functions smoothly and discusses a direct empirical-residual implementation without developing its complete first-order inference theory. We tackle this gap in a finite-dimensional nonlinear structural autoregression with unrestricted continuous marginal innovation distributions and a fixed normal-rank shock. Our estimator replaces each innovation quantile function with the empirical quantile of generated structural residuals and iterates the same structural transition. For any fixed collection of responses, we establish a joint \sqrt{T} asymptotic linear representation with four components: direct transition estimation, the effect of transition estimation on residual order statistics, ordinary innovation-quantile estimation, and the shifted impact quantile. After projection through the recursion, the quantile terms admit a residual-rank-and-spacing representation, yielding feasible inference without innovation-density estimation or quantile smoothing. We then characterize the propagated bias from smoothing, establish validity of a full recursive residual bootstrap, and derive the additional covariance contribution from a finite number of simulated paths, providing bandwidth-free inference for the empirical-residual version of the same normal-rank response used in the smooth recursive construction.

</details>


### [333] [A fully nonlinear structural vector autoregressive model identified via independent innovation analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03486)
*Savi Virolainen*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全非线性的结构向量自回归（SVAR）框架，其中同期结构映射可以是非线性和非加性的。通过利用观测外生变量引起的结构冲击条件分布的变化，结合对比学习框架和指数族结构来识别冲击，并使用前馈神经网络估计模型。实证应用研究美国工业生产对实际油价冲击的响应不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的独立创新分析（ICA）结果只能将结构冲击识别到任意分量可逆变换的程度，这对于结构计量经济分析通常是不够的。需要更强化的识别条件来确保冲击的可识别性，从而支持完全非线性的结构分析。

Method: 1) 提出完全非线性的SVAR框架，同期结构映射允许非线性和非加性；2) 利用观测外生变量引起的结构冲击条件分布变化实现识别；3) 采用对比学习框架结合指数族结构恢复冲击；4) 施加结构化指数族规范，将识别模糊性减少到每个冲击的一参数变换尺度映射；5) 进一步通过逻辑（logistic）规范强化识别到置换和分量符号变化；6) 使用前馈神经网络估计模型，利用其通用逼近能力。

Result: 实证研究发现美国工业生产对实际油价冲击的响应存在适度的不对称性，这种不对称性与冲击的符号和经济状态有关。论文还提供了R包iiasvar来实现所介绍的方法。

Conclusion: 通过施加结构化指数族和逻辑规范，成功将非线性SVAR中结构冲击的识别从任意分量变换加强到置换和符号变化级别，实现了完全非线性SVAR的可行估计，并在油价冲击对工业产出的不对称效应中得到了实证验证。

Abstract: We develop a fully nonlinear structural vector autoregressive framework in which the contemporaneous structural mapping may be nonlinear and non-additive. Identification is achieved by exploiting variation in the conditional distributions of the mutually independent structural shocks induced by an observed exogenous variable. Specifically, a general contrastive learning framework that makes use of this variation together with the assumed exponential-family structure is employed to recover the shocks. Existing independent innovation analysis results identify such shocks only up to arbitrary componentwise invertible transformations, which is generally insufficient for structural econometric analysis. We strengthen this result by imposing a structured exponential-family specification for the conditional shock distributions. With the imposed sufficient statistics, the remaining ambiguity is reduced to a one-parameter transformed-scale map for each shock. We then show that, under a logistic specification used for the natural parameters, the identification is further strengthened up to permutation and componentwise sign changes. Once the shocks have been recovered, the fully nonlinear structural vector autoregression can be estimated using feed-forward neural networks, motivated by their universal approximation capabilities. The empirical application studies asymmetries in the responses of U.S. industrial production to the real oil price shock. We find modest asymmetries with respect to the sign of the shock and state of the economy. The accompanying R package iiasvar implements the introduced methods.

</details>


### [334] [Testing for Smooth Structural Change in Cointegrated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03773)
*Haofeng Liao,Xing Wang*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出一个计量经济学框架，用于分析已知干预时间后协整系统中的平滑结构变化。采用向量误差修正模型（VECM），通过核加权局部降秩方法估计干预后平滑演化的调整系数和协整向量，并提供了参数一致性和平滑性的检验。实证应用于2022年2月24日俄罗斯入侵乌克兰后的能源、外汇和黄金市场，发现布伦特原油价格、美元/欧元汇率和黄金指数之间的协整关系在战争爆发后发生了平滑演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统协整分析假设长期关系在样本期内恒定不变，但现实中的重大事件（如战争、政策干预）可能导致经济系统结构发生变化。现有方法多关注离散的体制转换，缺乏对干预后协整结构平滑演化的系统分析框架。

Method: 在VECM框架下，分解Π(δ)=α(δ)β(δ)'，将长期均衡关系（β）与短期调整速度（α）分离开来。利用干预前的稳定子样本识别协整秩和协整结构；干预后，允许调整系数和协整向量作为缩放时间的平滑函数演化，通过核加权局部降秩方法估计。同时构建两个检验：参数一致性检验和干预后参数平滑性检验。

Result: 实证分析表明，在2022年2月24日俄罗斯入侵乌克兰后，布伦特原油价格、USD/EUR即期汇率和Credit Suisse NASDAQ黄金价格指数之间的协整关系发生了平滑变化，而非保持干预前的恒定长期协整系统，同时也区别于离散的体制转换。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效区分离散体制转换与平滑的干预后演化，为分析重大事件对长期经济关系的影响提供了新的工具。实证结果显示乌克兰战争对能源、外汇和黄金市场间的长期均衡关系产生了持久的平滑影响。

Abstract: This paper develops an econometric framework for analysing smooth structural change in cointegrated systems following a known intervention time. We consider a vector error-correction model in which the cointegration rank and the pre-intervention cointegrating structure are identified from a stable pre-intervention subsample. After the intervention, both the adjustment coefficients and the cointegrating vectors are allowed to evolve smoothly as functions of rescaled time, which are estimated using kernel-weighted local reduced-rank methods. The analysis is formulated directly in a cointegrated VAR/VECM system, which preserves the treatment of long-run relations and short-run error-correction dynamics. By working with the decomposition $Π(δ)=α(δ)β(δ)'$, the method separates changes in the equilibrium relation from those in the speed of adjustment. We also provide two tests for the parameter consistency and the post-intervention parameter smoothness respectively. An empirical application to energy market, foreign-exchange, and gold-market index around the 24 February 2022 Russia's invasion of Ukraine illustrates how the proposed approach distinguishes between a discrete regime shift and smooth post-intervention evolution. The results suggest that cointegrating relation among the price of Brent crude oil, the spot exchange rate (USD/EUR), and the Credit Suisse NASDAQ Gold Price Index has smoothly changed after the outbreak of war, instead of a constant long-run conintegration system in the pre-intervention period.

</details>


### [335] [Identification and Information after Nuisance Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03847)
*Ulrich Hounyo*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究线性面板工具变量（IV）在去除固定效应、潜因子或高维控制变量（即"干扰投影"）后的识别与推断问题。文章提出了投影雅可比矩阵、投影得分定律和投影信息矩阵三个核心工具，建立了弱识别极限、可行的因子转移条件、识别稳健检验和引导程序等理论框架，并通过模拟与国际货币应用验证了"识别必须在干扰移除后评估"的核心观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究中常通过去除固定效应、潜因子或高维控制变量来减少混淆，但这种变换在降低混杂的同时也可能消除识别方差。现有的推断框架未系统处理在双向依赖（two-way dependence）下经过一次方程兼容干扰投影（equation-compatible nuisance projection）后的线性面板IV识别问题，导致研究者在干扰移除后难以正确评估识别强度。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为干扰投影后的识别与推断提供完整框架。

Method: 本文发展了一套三维度的理论工具：1）投影雅可比矩阵（projected Jacobian）用于判断干扰移除后哪些结构方向仍然可见；2）投影得分定律（projected-score law）用于决定这些可见方向的估计精度；3）在高斯固定秩层（Gaussian fixed-rank strata）上，前两者结合形成投影信息矩阵（Projected Information Matrix）。在此基础上，作者推导了维度特定信息积累的弱识别极限、可行的因子转移条件、识别稳健检验、针对非高斯交互极限的引导程序，以及投影谱、秩、子空间和信息矩阵的推断方法。

Result: 模拟结果表明，原始第一阶段统计量超过500时仍可能支持错误的符号，而投影诊断能够揭示弱的有效信息。在国际货币应用中，常见投影显著削弱了表面上显著的外国产出持续性，而高斯参考Anderson-Rubin置信集仍然无界，说明识别信息在投影后严重不足。

Conclusion: 识别必须在干扰移除之后进行评估，而不是在原始数据上进行。传统只看原始第一阶段统计量的做法可能产生误导，投影后的诊断工具（如投影雅可比矩阵和投影信息矩阵）才是评估识别有效性的可靠依据。

Abstract: Empirical work often removes fixed effects, latent factors, or high-dimensional controls before estimating structural relationships. These transformations reduce confounding but may also remove identifying variation. We study linear panel IV after one equation-compatible nuisance projection under two-way dependence. The projected Jacobian determines which structural directions remain visible; the projected-score law determines their precision; and, on Gaussian fixed-rank strata, they combine in a Projected Information Matrix. We derive weak-identification limits with dimension-specific information accumulation, feasible factor-transfer conditions, identification-robust tests, bootstrap procedures for non-Gaussian interaction limits, and inference for the projected spectrum, rank, subspaces, and information matrix. Simulations show that a raw first-stage statistic above 500 can support the wrong sign while projected diagnostics reveal weak valid information. In an international monetary application, common projection substantially attenuates apparent foreign-output persistence, while Gaussian-reference Anderson--Rubin sets remain unbounded. Identification should therefore be assessed after nuisance removal.

</details>


### [336] [Difference-in-differences with "bad controls"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03881)
*Carolina Caetano,Brantly Callaway,Stroud Payne,Hugo Sant'Anna*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究了双重差分（DID）识别策略中，当平行趋势假设需要以可能受处理影响的协变量（即"坏控制变量"）为条件时的问题。作者提出了两种替代方法：一是基于处理前值的Callaway和Sant'Anna估计量，二是在协变量无混淆条件下使用插补和双重/去偏机器学习估计量，并扩展到交错处理采用情境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DID方法在处理"坏控制变量"（受处理影响的协变量）时面临困境：直接丢弃这些变量往往不合适，因为它们在平行趋势假设中可能至关重要；但直接将其作为协变量又可能导致估计偏差。现有文献缺乏系统的解决方案，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: （1）推导出仅以坏控制变量的处理前值作为条件的显式条件，自然地导向使用处理前值作为协变量的Callaway和Sant'Anna（2021）估计量；（2）在协变量无混淆条件下，开发插补估计量和双重/去偏机器学习（DML）估计量来恢复处理组的平均处理效应（ATT）；（3）将结果扩展到交错处理采用情形，并提供识别假设的预检验。

Result: 本文为"坏控制变量"问题提供了两种可行的估计策略，给出了识别条件的显式推导，并将方法推广到更复杂的交错处理采用场景，同时提供了假设的预检验工具。该方法被应用于研究失业对收入的影响。

Conclusion: 作者认为简单地丢弃坏控制变量通常不可取，而本文提出的两种方法（基于处理前值的DID和基于无混淆条件的机器学习方法）能够在坏控制存在的情况下有效恢复ATT，为实证研究者提供了实用的替代方案。

Abstract: This paper considers difference-in-differences identification strategies when the parallel trends assumption holds after conditioning on covariates that may themselves be affected by the treatment (often referred to as "bad controls"). We show that common approaches such as simply dropping bad controls are often ill-advised and develop two alternative approaches that allow bad controls to function as genuine controls despite being affected by treatment. First, we derive explicit conditions that rationalize conditioning only on pre-treatment values of the bad control, leading naturally to the Callaway and Sant'Anna (2021) estimator with pre-treatment values as covariates. Second, under a covariate unconfoundedness condition, we develop imputation and double/debiased machine learning estimators that recover the average treatment effect on the treated. We extend these results to staggered treatment adoption, provide pre-tests for the identifying assumptions, and apply the methods to study the effects of job displacement on earnings.

</details>


### [337] [When Predictions Become Regressors: A Split-Sample Correction for Biases in Downstream Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02909)
*Nathan Canen,Ted Enamorado*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解决预测生成变量（如LLM产生的测量）在回归分析中因测量误差导致估计偏倚的简单方法：利用原始数据独立分割产生的多个测量构造工具变量，该方法理论上有效、易于实施且无需新数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于预测的方法（包括LLM和其他机器学习技术）常被用于构造难以直接量化的政治现象测量指标，如政党宣言中的政策立场或社交媒体情绪。这些预测生成的测量常作为回归模型中的解释变量使用，但存在测量误差，会导致估计偏倚。现有方法未能简单有效地解决这一偏倚问题。

Method: 该方法利用原始数据的独立分割（independent splits）创建多个测量，并用这些多样化的测量构造工具变量（instrumental variables）。这种方法在理论上有效，易于实施，且不需要额外收集新数据。通过模拟研究验证了方法的有效性，并将其应用于两个实际案例：德国议会中性别化言论对立法结果的影响，以及中国政治风险对扶贫项目的影响。

Result: 模拟研究表明，即使在相对较小的样本中，该方法也能恢复接近真实值的估计，而标准方法在实践中可能产生显著的偏倚。两个实际应用案例（德国议会和中国扶贫项目）进一步验证了该方法的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于独立分割构造工具变量的方法为解决预测生成变量的测量误差偏倚提供了一种简单、可行且有效的方案，为使用LLM等预测方法生成政治学测量指标的实证研究提供了重要的方法论贡献。

Abstract: Prediction-based methods, including Large Language Models (LLMs) and other machine learning techniques, are often used to construct measures of political phenomena that are difficult to quantify directly, such as policy positions in manifestos or emotions expressed on social media. In many applications, these prediction-generated measures are used as explanatory variables in regression models, even though they are measured with error. This leads to biased estimates. In this paper, we propose a simple solution to these biases: instrumental variables constructed from multiple measures created on independent splits of the original data. This approach is theoretically valid, easy to implement, and does not require new data. Through simulations, we show that this approach recovers estimates close to the true values, even in relatively small samples, while the standard approach can produce substantial bias in practice. We illustrate the method by revisiting two applications: whether gendered speech affects legislative outcomes in the German Parliament, and whether political risk influences poverty alleviation programs in China.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [338] [Stochastic Saddle Avoidance Beyond Unit Excitation and Smoothness: A Pathwise Lyapunov-Perron Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03001)
*Junwen Qiu,Bohao Ma,Andre Milzarek,Junyu Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需单元激励（UE）假设的随机递归严格鞍点回避定理，通过可验证的路径条件替代UE条件，并应用于随机镜像下降、随机重排及近端随机梯度方法，证明其能几乎必然回避严格鞍点并收敛到局部极小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单元激励（UE）假设要求随机误差在每个方向上都有均匀正分量，这虽然能直接排除收敛到严格鞍点，但过度简化了实际噪声结构。在过参数化/插值模型中噪声可能消失，在有限和问题中噪声可能位于低维子空间，这些常见场景都不满足UE条件。因此需要一个更普适的鞍点回避理论框架。

Method: 作者证明了不依赖UE的抽象几乎必然鞍点回避定理，用可验证的路径条件替代UE要求。由于随机采样的映射通常不共享固定点，经典的确定性中心稳定流形论证不再适用，因此采用路径依赖的变量替换结合基于路径的Lyapunov-Perron证明策略。

Result: 获得了随机镜像下降（含SGD）和随机重排（random reshuffling）的严格鞍点回避结果；对于非光滑复合目标函数，证明了近端型随机梯度方法的回避结果。结合迭代收敛保证，可建立收敛到原始目标函数局部极小值的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作为不满足UE条件的主流随机优化场景（如过参数化、插值模型、有限和问题）提供了严格的鞍点回避理论基础，扩展了随机优化收敛到局部极小值的适用性范围。

Abstract: Unit excitation (UE) is a common assumption in stochastic saddle avoidance: the stochastic error must have a uniformly positive component along every direction, in expectation. This condition gives a direct way to rule out convergence to strict saddles, but it also oversimplifies the actual noise structure, and does not match many stochastic optimization regimes. In overparameterized or interpolation models, the noise may vanish near stationarity. In finite-sum problems, the stochastic gradient noise may lie in a low-dimensional, data-dependent subspace. In these (common) scenarios, UE is naturally not satisfied. In this paper, we prove an abstract almost sure avoidance theorem for stochastic recursions without UE. The theorem replaces UE-type requirements by verifiable pathwise conditions. In applications, these conditions follow, e.g., from local smoothness and finite-moment assumptions under standard i.i.d. sampling, or from the finite-sum structure under without-replacement sampling. Since the stochastically sampled maps generally do not share a fixed point, the celebrated center-stable manifold argument used in deterministic analyses is not directly applicable. Instead, we use a path-dependent change of variables together with a pathwise Lyapunov--Perron-based proof strategy. As applications, we obtain strict saddle avoidance for stochastic mirror descent (including SGD) and for random reshuffling. For nonsmooth composite objectives, we prove avoidance results for a proximal-type stochastic gradient method. Combining these insights with suitable iterate convergence guarantees, this allows establishing convergence to local minimizers of the original objective function.

</details>


### [339] [Oracle-Based Distributionally Robust Optimization under Optimal Transport Ambiguity Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03075)
*Guixian Chen,Salar Fattahi,Soroosh Shafiee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解最优传输DRO问题的高效算法，通过将内层最坏情况期望问题精确简化为标量预算分配任务，避免了传统大规模凸重构方法中大量辅助变量和锥约束带来的计算瓶颈，并在数值实验中显著优于现有最先进求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DRO问题通过将minimax问题重构为单层凸规划来求解，虽然理论上可行，但引入大量辅助变量和苛刻的锥约束，导致实际计算性能差、扩展性差。作者希望克服这一计算瓶颈，提出更高效的求解方案。

Method: (1) 将内层最坏情况期望问题精确简化为标量预算分配任务；(2) 基于此设计高效算法，绕过大规模提升重构；(3) 提供快速后处理方案恢复最优最坏情况分布（支撑点最多N+1个）；(4) 将算法嵌入基于oracle的分布最佳响应框架中，直接计算DRO问题的近似原始-对偶解；(5) 将对偶DRO分析扩展到支持至多min{N+n+1, KN}个原子的最不利分布，并提供高效凸规划约简来提取。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的方法在性能上显著优于基于重构的最先进求解器。

Conclusion: 通过结构性的洞察将内层问题简化为标量任务，并提供对偶侧的最不利分布支撑点理论保证，本文展示了在DRO求解效率和可扩展性上的实质性提升，为大规模鲁棒优化问题提供了可行的计算路径。

Abstract: Distributionally robust optimization (DRO) with optimal transport ambiguity sets is traditionally solved by reformulating the minimax problem into a single-level convex program. While theoretically tractable, these reformulations introduce numerous auxiliary variables and demanding conic constraints that scale poorly in practice. In this paper, we address this challenge by reducing the inner worst-case expectation problem exactly to a scalar budget allocation task. This structural insight yields an efficient algorithm that bypasses large lifted reformulations, alongside a fast post-processing scheme to recover an optimal worst-case distribution supported on at most $N+1$ points, where $N$ denotes the sample size. We embed this procedure within an oracle-based distributional best-response framework to directly compute an approximate primal-dual solution to the overall DRO problem. Furthermore, we extend our analysis to the dual DRO formulation, proving the existence of a least-favorable distribution supported on at most $\min\{N+n+1, KN\}$ atoms, where $n$ and $K$ denote the decision dimension and number of loss components, respectively, and provide an efficient convex programming reduction to extract it from the solution of the primal DRO. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art reformulation-based solvers.

</details>


### [340] [Optimal Nonergodic Primal-Dual Complexity of Efficient Inexact Parameter-Free Augmented Lagrangian Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03170)
*Arnesh Sujanani,Saeed Ghadimi,Henry Wołkowicz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对线性约束凸复合优化问题，提出三种非精确增广拉格朗日(AL)方法，实现最优原始-对偶复杂度O(ε⁻¹)（强凸情形下为O(ε⁻¹/²log(ε⁻¹))），优于现有PAL等方法，并在六个问题类别上验证了5-50倍的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非精确增广拉格朗日方法在凸优化中的复杂度界（如O(ε⁻⁴/³)、O(ε⁻⁷/⁴)、O(ε⁻²)）不够理想，且PAL方法仅有O(ε⁻¹log(ε⁻¹))的保证。同时，许多方法依赖不可验证的目标值误差，缺乏可验证的近似KKT解保证。作者希望开发具有最优复杂度且可验证KKT解的算法。

Method: 提出三种非精确增广拉格朗日(AL)方案：(1) 针对凸复合优化问题（光滑凸函数+紧致域上非光滑闭凸函数），设计基于可验证近似KKT解的迭代方案；(2) 三种凸变体中有两种是无参数(parameter-free)的；(3) 建立了非遍历收敛保证，其中一种变体具有更强的最后迭代(last-iterate)保证；(4) 在强凸情形下实现近最优复杂度。

Result: 凸情形下达到最优原始-对偶复杂度O(ε⁻¹)，优于先前AL方法（O(ε⁻⁴/³)、O(ε⁻⁷/⁴)、O(ε⁻²)）以及PAL方法的O(ε⁻¹log(ε⁻¹))；强凸情形下达到近最优复杂度O(ε⁻¹/²log(ε⁻¹))。在六个重要问题类别（弹性网最小二乘回归、组稀疏Huber化SVM、量子半定规划等）上，相比代表性PAL方法实现5-50倍加速。

Conclusion: 本文提出的非精确AL方法在理论上达到最优复杂度，且无需参数调整，在实践中相比PAL方法有显著的计算优势，为线性约束凸优化的非精确增广拉格朗日方法提供了更优的理论与实证结果。

Abstract: We develop inexact augmented Lagrangian (AL) methods for linearly constrained convex composite optimization problems whose objective is the sum of a smooth convex function and a possibly nonsmooth closed proper convex function with compact domain. Unlike primal accuracy guarantees based on objective-value error, our methods target verifiable approximate KKT solutions. In the convex setting, we propose three inexact AL schemes with optimal primal-dual complexity $\mathcal O(ε^{-1})$, improving prior AL bounds such as $\mathcal O(ε^{-4/3})$, $\mathcal O(ε^{-7/4})$, and $\mathcal O(ε^{-2})$, and improving the $\mathcal O(ε^{-1}\log(ε^{-1}))$ guarantees of proximal augmented Lagrangian (PAL) methods. Two of the three convex variants are parameter-free. We also establish non-ergodic convergence guarantees, including a stronger last-iterate guarantee for one variant. In the strongly convex setting, our methods achieve near-optimal complexity $\mathcal O(ε^{-1/2}\log(ε^{-1}))$, with two parameter-free variants. Numerical experiments on six important problem classes, including elastic-net least-squares regression, group-sparse Huberized support vector machines, and a quantum semidefinite program (SDP), demonstrate substantial computational advantages of our inexact AL methods over a representative PAL method, with speedups frequently ranging from $5$ to $50$ times.

</details>


### [341] [Optimal Deterministic Oracle Complexity for Weakly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03246)
*Jiajin Li,Siyu Pan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在ρ-弱凸和G-Lipschitz函数上寻找ε-稳定点的最优确定性一阶预言机复杂度，证明了其下界为Ω(ρG²Δ/ε⁴)，与已知的上界匹配，确立了确定性一阶预言机的最优复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在确定非光滑弱凸优化中寻找ε-稳定点所需的确定性一阶预言机查询次数的下界，以理解非光滑几何对优化复杂度产生的本质影响，并揭示其与光滑非凸优化之间的复杂度分离。

Method: 通过构造特定类型的ρ-弱凸和G-Lipschitz函数，证明任何确定性一阶算法（每次查询可同时获得函数值和完整次微分）都需要Ω(ρG²Δ/ε⁴)次查询，利用信息论和对抗性论证建立下限。

Result: 证明了确定性一阶算法的下界Ω(ρG²Δ/ε⁴)（在Δ≤G²/ρ条件下），该下界与已知的确定性和随机一阶上限匹配至常数因子，确立了最优确定性预言机复杂度。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了光滑非凸优化（Θ(ε⁻²)复杂度）与非光滑弱凸优化（Θ(ε⁻⁴)复杂度）之间存在本质的复杂度分离，额外的ε⁻²因子源于非光滑几何特性而非随机性。

Abstract: We study the oracle complexity of finding $ε$-stationary points of $ρ$-weakly convex and $G$-Lipschitz functions, where stationarity is measured by the gradient of the Moreau envelope. We consider a first-order oracle that returns both the function value and the full subdifferential at every query point. We prove that every deterministic first-order algorithm requires $ Ω({ρG^2Δ}/{ε^4})$ oracle queries whenever $Δ\leq {G^2}/ρ$, where $f(\bz)-\inf f \leq Δ$. This lower bound matches the best known deterministic and stochastic first-order upper bounds, up to universal constants, and establishes the optimal deterministic oracle complexity. The result reveals a fundamental complexity separation between smooth nonconvex and nonsmooth weakly convex optimization. While smooth nonconvex minimization admits a $Θ(ε^{-2})$ oracle complexity, nonsmooth weakly convex optimization incurs an intrinsic additional $ε^{-2}$ factor arising from nonsmooth geometry rather than stochasticity.

</details>


### [342] [Joint-Range Inequalities for Nonconvex QCQPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03318)
*Liding Xu,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过"投影-再提升"方法为非凸二次约束二次规划（QCQP）生成割平面的新框架。该方法基于两条基有效不等式，投影到一个二维集合上进行分析，并给出非凸联合值域的闭式凸包描述以及凸联合值域的半定表示，从而产生新的联合值域不等式族。该方法受混合整数舍入（MIR）不等式启发，能够处理混合术语，并保持稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸QCQP问题具有广泛的应用，但求解困难。现有的割平面方法（如MIR不等式）主要针对混合整数线性问题，而针对非凸QCQP的割平面方法相对有限。作者希望通过类比MIR不等式的思路，设计能够更好暴露和利用非凸联合值域的割平面，从而增强QCQP的松弛效果。

Method: 首先，对扩展QCQP公式中的两条基有效不等式，将相关的两行松弛投影到二维集合。然后分析两个基不等式中二次函数的联合值域：对于非凸联合值域，给出闭式凸包描述；对于凸联合值域，给出半定表示。由此产生新的联合值域不等式族，再将其提升回扩展QCQP公式。此外，提出更灵活的割线混合联合值域不等式，以更好地处理混合项。该方法通过控制基不等式支撑集来保持稀疏性。

Result: 在初步几何实验中，联合值域不等式相较于通过重新线性化技术（RLT）构造的投影松弛，实现了显著的面积缩减。

Conclusion: 本文提出的联合值域不等式为求解非凸QCQP提供了新的一类割平面，具有闭式凸包描述、保持稀疏性、以及能处理混合项的优势，初步实验表明其能显著收紧松弛，是对现有MIR不等式思想在非凸QCQP上的有效推广。

Abstract: We study cutting planes for nonconvex quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQPs) through a project-then-lift approach inspired by mixed-integer rounding (MIR) inequalities. Given two base valid inequalities for the extended QCQP formulation, we project the associated two-row relaxation into a two-dimensional set and analyze the joint range of quadratic functions in two base inequalities. For the nonconvex joint range, we give a closed-form convex hull description of the projected set; for the convex joint range, we give its semidefinite representation. This yields a new family of joint-range inequalities, which can be lifted back to the extended QCQP formulation. MIR inequalities can handle ``mixed'' terms: continuous variables or fractional linear combinations of integer variables. Similarly, we propose more flexible secant mixed-joint-range inequalities, which better expose and exploit the nonconvex joint range. The proposed approach preserves sparsity, since the support of each lifted inequality is controlled by that of the base inequalities. In preliminary geometric experiments, the joint-range inequalities yield substantial area reduction of the projected relaxation constructed via reformulation-linearization-technique.

</details>


### [343] [Dynamic Proximal Point Method for Unconstrained Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03349)
*Enrico Bertolazzi,Alberto De Marchi,Davide Stocco*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于无约束优化的新型动态近端点算法。该算法通过自适应更新的对角矩阵加权二次正则项，使用内层牛顿法配合线搜索求解每个近端子问题，并在外层根据内层求解器性能调整参考点和正则化参数。论文推导了牛顿步的简化线性系统、定义了相应的价值函数，并讨论了从导数信息构造对角缩放矩阵的实用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的近端点算法通常使用固定的正则化参数或简单的缩放方式，这可能导致收敛速度慢或数值性能不佳。本文旨在通过动态调整对角正则化矩阵来提升近端点算法的收敛效率和实用性。

Method: 该方法包含两层结构：外层采用自适应更新的对角加权矩阵构造近端子问题，并依据内层求解器表现调整参考点与正则化参数；内层使用牛顿法与线搜索求解子问题以保证全局收敛。论文还推导了牛顿步的简化线性系统、定义了价值函数，并给出了构造对角缩放矩阵的实用方案及实现伪代码。

Result: 论文提供了完整的算法框架，包括简化线性系统的推导、价值函数的定义、基于导数信息的对角缩放矩阵构造方法，以及与所提方法一致的实现伪代码和停机准则。

Conclusion: 该动态近端点算法通过自适应对角正则化机制，在保证全局收敛的同时提升实际计算效率，为无约束优化提供了一种新颖且实用可行的求解框架。

Abstract: In this work, we present a novel dynamic proximal point algorithm for unconstrained optimization. The method generates a sequence of proximal subproblems, where the quadratic regularization term is weighted by a diagonal matrix that is updated adaptively at each iteration. Each subproblem is solved using an inner Newton's method combined with a line search, which provides a global convergence mechanism for the nonlinear solver. At the outer level, the algorithm updates the reference point and adjusts the regularization parameter based on the performance of the inner Newton solver. We derive the reduced linear system used to compute the Newton step, define the corresponding merit function, and discuss practical approaches for constructing the diagonal scaling matrix from derivative information. The paper also provides implementation-oriented pseudocode and stopping criteria that are consistent with the proposed method.

</details>


### [344] [Fast and slow mean-field games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03376)
*Roxana Dumitrescu,Julian Gutierrez Pineda,Peter Tankov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于双时间尺度结构的框架，用于构造带公共噪声的均值场博弈（MFG）的近似纳什均衡。公共噪声由快变量承载（遍历动力学），慢变量被最优控制或停止。核心思想是利用一个"有效"的无公共噪声MFG的均衡来近似全MFG的均衡，其中系数相对于快尺度过程的平稳测度进行平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带公共噪声的均值场博弈通常比无公共噪声的情况在数学处理上更加复杂和困难。本文的动机是希望通过将带公共噪声的复杂MFG问题简化为一个"有效"的无公共噪声MFG问题，从而降低求解难度，同时保证近似的精度可控。

Method: 方法包括：（1）构建一个双时间尺度结构，其中公共噪声由快速遍历动力学承载；（2）将系数关于快尺度过程的平稳测度进行平均，得到"有效"MFG；（3）从有效MFG的均衡出发，通过随机化控制和停止构造显式的ε-MFG均衡；（4）利用两尺度扩散过程的强收敛结果，证明时间尺度分离参数控制纳什均衡条件的误差。

Result: 本文获得了带随机停止的MFG均衡存在性的新结果，并证明了从有效MFG均衡构造的近似均衡在纳什均衡条件中的误差可由时间尺度分离参数控制，从而建立了ε-MFG均衡的显式构造方法。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一种将带公共噪声的复杂MFG问题降维为无公共噪声的有效MFG问题的系统性框架，并给出了可量化的近似精度保证，为实际应用中处理带公共噪声的MFG提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We propose a framework for constructing approximate Nash equilibria in mean-field games (MFG) with common noise based on a two-time-scale structure. In our model, the common noise is carried by a fast variable evolving under ergodic dynamics, while the slow variable is either optimally controlled or stopped. The fast variable enters the dynamics of the slow one through the drift coefficient. The key idea is to construct an approximation to the solution of the full MFG with common noise using an ``effective'' MFG without common noise, where the coefficients are averaged with respect to the stationary measure of the fast-scale process. We construct an explicit $\varepsilon$-MFG equilibrium for the full MFG from the equilibrium for the effective MFG with randomized control and stopping. To this end, we obtain new results on existence of MFG equilibria with randomized stopping. We rely on strong convergence results for two-scale diffusions under structural assumptions on the MFG, and show that the time-scale separation parameter controls the error in the Nash equilibrium condition.

</details>


### [345] [A proximal difference of convex functions algorithm using Barzilai-Borwein step size with nonmonotone line search and extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03465)
*Kelin Wu,Hongpeng Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的近端差分之凸（DC）算法框架，用于求解一般非凸、非光滑优化问题。通过结合Barzilai-Borwein（BB）步长与非单调线搜索策略，克服了标准近端DC算法中步长保守和稳定性不足的问题；同时开发外推机制加速收敛并保证全局稳定性。在Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz性质下严格建立了全局收敛性。数值实验表明，该方法在SCAD正则化最小二乘问题和图形Ginzburg-Landau图像分割模型上具有高度竞争力的效率和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准近端DC算法在处理非凸非光滑优化问题时，存在步长选择保守导致收敛速度慢、以及数值稳定性不足的问题，亟需一种能够加速收敛同时保证全局稳定性的算法框架。

Method: 提出一种近端DC算法框架，将Barzilai-Borwein（BB）步长与非单调线搜索策略相结合，并引入外推机制以加速收敛，在Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz性质下建立全局收敛性保证。

Result: 在SCAD正则化最小二乘问题和图形Ginzburg-Landau图像分割模型的数值实验中，所提方法在效率和精度上均优于现有DC算法，展现出高度竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了近端DC算法的步长保守和稳定性问题，在保持全局收敛性的前提下显著提升了算法性能，为非凸非光滑优化问题提供了更优的求解方案。

Abstract: The paper proposes a novel proximal difference-of-convex (DC) algorithmic framework to solve general non-convex, non-smooth optimization problems. By combining Barzilai-Borwein (BB) step sizes with nonmonotone line search strategies, our approach effectively overcomes the conservative step sizes and stability issues inherent in standard proximal DC algorithms. Furthermore, we develop extrapolation mechanisms to accelerate convergence while ensuring global stability. The global convergence of the proposed algorithms is rigorously established under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz property. Numerical experiments on the SCAD-regularized least squares problem and graphic Ginzburg-Landau image segmentation models demonstrate that the proposed methods achieve highly competitive efficiency and accuracy compared to existing DC algorithms.

</details>


### [346] [Pursuing Optimal Stepsize in Adaptive Gradient-Based Quadratic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03546)
*Yifan Wang,Luca Ballotta,Ruggero Carli,Xianghui Cao,Luca Schenato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需先验全局函数参数的梯度下降自适应步长算法，通过运行局部曲率的最小值和最大值估计来快速收敛，并证明该算法能够收敛到最优常数步长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于二次函数的梯度下降，传统最优步长依赖于全局函数参数（如Lipschitz常数和强凸参数）的先验知识，而在实际应用中这些参数通常是未知的。因此需要一种无需先验参数、计算轻量且能快速收敛的自适应方法。

Method: 提出一种基于运行估计的局部最小和最大曲率的自适应步长策略。该方法受平滑凸函数自适应步长算法的启发，通过在线估计局部曲率来动态调整步长，并且计算开销很低。

Result: 理论证明所提算法能够收敛到实现最快收敛速度的最优常数步长。仿真实验表明，在二次函数情形下以及逻辑回归分类的初步测试中，该算法的收敛速度与近期自适应方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: 该自适应步长方法在无需全局参数先验知识的前提下，不仅能保证收敛到最优步长，还在实际应用中展现出与或优于现有自适应方法的性能，具有计算轻量且实用的优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of achieving fast convergence in gradient descent for quadratic functions without relying on a priori knowledge of global function parameters. Inspired by adaptive stepsize algorithms for smooth convex functions, we propose a computationally lightweight strategy based on running estimates of minimal and maximal local curvatures. We prove that our proposed algorithm converges to the optimal constant stepsize which achieves the fastest convergence. Simulations show that the convergence rate achieved by our proposed algorithm is comparable or superior to recent adaptive approaches both in the quadratic case under consideration and in a preliminary test on logistic classification.

</details>


### [347] [Adaptive Stepsizes With Certified Convergence in Distributed Gradient Tracking With Quadratic Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03548)
*Yifan Wang,Luca Ballotta,Ruggero Carli,Andrea Iannelli,Xianghui Cao,Luca Schenato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对标量二次问题中的分布式梯度追踪算法，提出了一种自适应步长规则，该规则仅依赖共识矩阵的谱半径和代价曲率异质性（两者均可在线计算），保证收敛且对完全图及同质曲率情形达到紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数分布式梯度算法需要合适的步长选择，但现有理论界往往过于保守，而实际实现依赖经验调参。在线自适应策略最近才出现，其性质和性能尚未被充分理解。研究者希望通过标量二次代价这一信息丰富的设定，获得分析性洞见。

Method: 聚焦标量二次代价问题，显式刻画网络拓扑与曲率异质性之间的相互作用；推导出仅由共识矩阵的本质谱半径和局部代价曲率异质性参数化的收敛界（两者均可在线计算，无需任何问题先验知识）；优化该界得到计算上易处理的收敛率代理及最优常数步长的代理。

Result: 所得到的步长具有解析解释，能对任意网络拓扑和曲率异质性保证收敛，并在完全图和同质曲率情形下被证明是紧的。大量数值模拟表明该方法显著优于现有离线和在线步长选择规则。

Conclusion: 该自适应步长规则在保证收敛的同时，仅需可在线计算的参数，并通过理论紧性和数值实验验证了其优越性，为分布式优化中的步长选择提供了新的理论依据和实用方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose an adaptive stepsize rule with guaranteed convergence for Distributed Gradient Tracking applied to scalar quadratic problems with heterogeneous curvatures. Most distributed gradient-based algorithms require a suitable stepsize selection. Available theoretical bounds are often overly conservative, while practical implementations typically rely on empirically tuned heuristics. Online adaptive strategies have only recently emerged for general distributed convex optimization, but their properties and performance remain only partially understood. To gain analytical insight, we focus on the informative setting of scalar quadratic costs, which allows us to explicitly capture the interplay between network topology and curvature heterogeneity. We derive a convergence bound parameterized only by the essential spectral radius of the consensus matrix and the heterogeneity of the local cost curvatures, both computable online without any prior knowledge of the optimization problem. Optimizing this bound yields a computationally tractable surrogate for the convergence rate and the optimal constant stepsize. The resulting stepsize admits an analytical interpretation, guarantees convergence for arbitrary network topologies and curvature heterogeneity, and is provably tight for complete graphs and homogeneous curvatures. Finally, extensive numerical simulations demonstrate that the proposed distributed adaptive strategy significantly outperforms existing offline and online stepsize selection rules in the considered setting.

</details>


### [348] [Sliding Methods for Hölder-Smooth Convex--Concave Minimax Optimization with Bilinear Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03846)
*Nhat Trung Nguyen,Alexander Gasnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有双线性耦合的凸-凹极小极大优化问题，提出了利用复合结构的滑动方法（sliding method），针对Hölder连续（次）梯度的f和g函数，实现了每个函数按照自身光滑度级别分别进行梯度评估，而非受限于两者中较差者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理大规模凸-凹极小极大问题时，往往受到两个函数中较差的函数性质（如光滑度）的限制，导致计算效率低下。本文旨在利用问题中的复合结构，设计一种方法使得每个函数的查询频率可以独立地由其自身性质决定，从而克服这种"最差者决定"的瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种基于单调变分不等式递归滑动方案的滑动方法。该方法分别以由f、g各自性质决定的频率查询它们的oracle，以及双线性耦合算子。利用f和g的Hölder连续性建立收敛保证，并分析了非强凸和部分强凸两种情形。对于随机问题，证明了退化情形下的均匀期望间隙界，以及在环境光滑性和正有效曲率下的噪声底收敛。

Result: 建立了在Hölder连续性下的收敛保证，复杂度界显式依赖于Hölder指数、Hölder常数、强凸参数和耦合矩阵的谱性质。数值实验验证了预测的Hölder指数，并确认每个函数的梯度评估次数按其自身光滑度分离。断层扫描基准显示了在梯度评估比矩阵向量乘更昂贵时的运行时间优势。

Conclusion: 该滑动方法成功地将函数的查询频率与其个体光滑度水平解耦，在包含非光滑到光滑的广泛问题类型中均有效，为大规模耦合极小极大优化提供了高效且理论完备的解决方案。

Abstract: We study convex-concave minimax optimization problems with bilinear coupling of the form $\min_{x\in \mathcal X}\max_{y\in \mathcal Y} \; f(x)+\langle y,\mathbf{B}x\rangle-g(y),$ where the functions $f$ and $g$ have Hölder continuous (sub)gradients. This setting covers a broad range of regimes, from nonsmooth problems with bounded subgradient variation to smooth problems with Lipschitz continuous gradients; for a smooth component used in the coupling-induced regularizer, its Lipschitz-gradient constant is assumed to hold in the ambient space. We propose a sliding method that exploits the composite structure of the problem by querying the oracles associated with $f$, $g$, and the bilinear coupling operator at prescribed frequencies determined by their individual properties. The method is based on a recursive sliding scheme for monotone variational inequalities. We establish convergence guarantees under Hölder continuity and show how the resulting complexity bounds depend explicitly on the Hölder exponents, Hölder constants, strong convexity parameters, and spectral properties of the coupling matrix. Our analysis covers nonstrongly convex and partially strongly convex regimes. For stochastic problems, we prove a uniform expected-gap bound in the degenerate regime and, under ambient smoothness and positive effective curvature, convergence up to an explicit noise floor. Numerical experiments reproduce the predicted Hölder exponents and confirm that the number of gradient evaluations required for each function separates according to its own smoothness level rather than the worse of the two. A tomographic benchmark shows runtime gains when gradient evaluations are more expensive than the additional matrix-vector products.

</details>


### [349] [A Robust Chance Constrained Approach to Surgery Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03931)
*Marco Caserta,Antonio García Romero,Miguel Vaquero*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个鲁棒机会约束框架用于择期手术排程，在手术时长不确定的情况下，将不确定性量化与排程优化解耦。缓冲引擎将分布信息转化为可靠性依赖的缓冲时长，排程模型联合决策分配、排序、开始时间以及各手术的可靠性级别（从离散菜单中选取），使可靠性成为内生的排程决策而非固定参数。在马德里HLA Moncloa医院的滚动案例研究中（覆盖10个实例、45至227台手术），该方法将超过90分钟的延误减少97%，第95百分位延误降低约620分钟，总加班时间减少42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于平均时长或固定缓冲规则的手术排程虽然在事前看似高效，但在实际执行中很脆弱——早期手术的超时会在一天中不断传导累积，使后续手术暴露在累积延误中。因此需要一种能更好权衡保守性与执行可靠性的排程方法。

Method: 提出鲁棒机会约束框架，分为两部分：(1) 缓冲引擎——将分布信息转化为可靠性依赖的缓冲时长；(2) 排程模型——联合优化手术分配、序列、开始时间以及各手术级别的可靠性（从离散菜单中选择）。框架支持平均可靠性、最差日和硬目标三种风险姿态，将可靠性作为内生的排程决策。

Result: 与常见的统一可靠性和均匀比例缓冲基准相比，该框架的价值来源于利用手术级别异质性，在最需要的地方分配保护，同时避免不必要的保守性。在HLA Moncloa医院的案例中，与确定性均值基线相比，超过90分钟的延误减少97%，第95百分位延误降低约620分钟，总加班时间减少42%。

Conclusion: 通过根据不确定性和运营暴露度来分配缓冲，该框架能将异质的时长数据和风险偏好转化为执行中更可靠且比一刀切规则更少保守的排程方案。

Abstract: We study elective surgery scheduling under uncertain procedure durations. Schedules based on mean durations or fixed buffering rules may appear efficient ex ante but become fragile in execution, as early overruns propagate through the day and expose later surgeries to accumulated delay. We propose a robust chance-constrained framework that separates uncertainty quantification from schedule optimization. A buffer engine converts distributional information into reliability-dependent buffered durations, while the scheduling model jointly selects assignments, sequences, start times, and surgery-level reliability levels from a discrete menu. Reliability therefore becomes an endogenous scheduling decision rather than a fixed service-level parameter. The framework accommodates average-reliability, worst-day, and hard-target risk postures. Comparisons with common-reliability and uniform proportional-buffer benchmarks show that the menu derives its value from exploiting surgery-level heterogeneity, allocating protection where it has the greatest operational value while avoiding unnecessary conservatism. In a rolling-origin case study at HLA Moncloa Hospital in Madrid, covering 10 instances with 45 to 227 surgeries, the approach reduces delays exceeding 90 minutes by 97%, lowers the 95th-percentile delay by approximately 620 minutes, and reduces total overtime by 42% relative to a deterministic mean-based baseline. By allocating buffers according to uncertainty and operational exposure, the framework translates heterogeneous duration data and risk preferences into schedules that are more reliable in execution and less conservatively buffered than one-size-fits-all rules.

</details>


### [350] [Simulation-Based Neural Policies for Portfolio Choice: Architecture, Training, and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03933)
*Jules Viard,Alexander Michaelides,Panos Parpas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究低维生命周期消费-储蓄与动态投资组合问题中，用神经网络策略替代传统动态规划网格求解的方法。作者在可精确求解的低维设定下，系统比较了四种神经网络架构（单网络、跨期拼接网络、按日期训练的逐期网络及其约束变体），并从福利、Bellman残差、形状约束和政策函数等多个维度进行联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维随机控制问题中，网格动态规划的代价随状态维度指数增长（维度灾难），因此需要用神经网络策略直接通过模拟优化。然而现有研究通常在高维场景下验证，由于缺乏参考解，无法隔离和诊断单一架构或训练选择的具体贡献，因此需要在低维可参考的场景下系统地研究架构与求解方法。

Method: 选取一个状态空间足够低维、可精确求解动态规划的生命周期问题作为基准，用精确解进行评估。比较四种架构：(1) 单一时间条件网络；(2) 跨制度切换拼接的两个网络；(3) 按日期训练、每日期一个网络并向后冻结下游策略；(4) 逐期架构的约束变体。训练采用方向主导优化（normalizes away gradient magnitude），使每个日期有短而适定目标。评估维度包括福利、无解的Bellman残差、形状限制及政策函数。

Result: 将策略在时间上解耦可使每个日期拥有短而适定的目标，配合方向主导优化效果良好。不同架构即使产生相似的已实现效用目标，在是否尊重底层问题的经济学性质上仍可能不同，因此需要多维联合评估。

Conclusion: 架构选择不仅影响数值效用，更影响解是否符合经济直觉与约束，应综合福利、Bellman残差、形状限制和政策函数多维度评估神经网络策略求解方法。

Abstract: Many economic decision problems, lifecycle consumption-saving and dynamic portfolio choice, are finite-horizon stochastic control problems with continuous states and actions. When the state is low-dimensional these problems are solved by dynamic programming on a grid. The grid cost grows exponentially in the state dimension, known as the curse of dimensionality, which motivates replacing the value-function grid with a neural policy optimized directly through simulation. Such policies are usually studied in the high-dimensional settings that motivate them, precisely where no reference solution exists. So the contribution of any single architectural or training choice cannot be isolated and diagnosed. We therefore take a step back and treat both the architecture and the solution method as the objects of study. To this end, we consider a lifecycle problem with a sufficiently low-dimensional normalized state space to admit an accurate dynamic programming solution, which is used for evaluation. We compare four architectures. The simplest consists of a single time-conditioned network. We then consider two networks concatenated across the regime switch, followed by one network per date trained backward against frozen downstream policies. Finally, we evaluate a constrained variant of the per-date architecture. Decoupling the policy across time gives each date a short, well-posed objective, which we pair with direction-dominant optimization that normalizes away gradient magnitude. Architectures that lead to similar realized utility objective can nevertheless differ in whether they respect the underlying problem's economics. We therefore evaluate each design jointly based on welfare, a solution-free Bellman residual, shape restrictions, and the resulting policy functions.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.RM'></div>

# q-fin.RM [[Back]](#toc)

### [351] [Preference robust distortion risk measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02854)
*Carole Bernard,Silvana M. Pesenti*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个偏好稳健决策框架，该框架针对风险偏好不确定性（而非分布不确定性）进行建模，通过广义扭曲风险度量、Wasserstein距离和Bregman散度构建模糊集，并推导出最坏/最好情形度量的闭式解，同时将框架扩展至等级依赖效用以解释Allais悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布鲁棒方法仅考虑收益分布的不确定性，而忽略了风险函数（即风险度量本身）的不确定性。实际决策中，决策者的风险偏好往往难以精确刻画，因此需要对风险泛函本身的不确定性进行建模，以实现更稳健的决策。

Method: 1) 利用Wasserstein距离和Bregman散度在扭曲（权重）函数上构建模糊集；2) 推导最坏情形与最好情形扭曲风险度量的闭式表达式；3) 将该框架扩展至等级依赖效用（RDU），将RDU视为期望效用模型的鲁棒化形式。

Result: 获得了偏好鲁棒扭曲风险度量的闭式解；发现等级依赖效用可从期望效用模型的鲁棒化过程中自然涌现，为解释Allais悖论提供了新颖的视角。

Conclusion: 该框架为风险偏好不确定下的决策提供了理论工具，将偏好稳健性与行为经济学中的经典悖论联系起来，开辟了偏好鲁棒决策的新方向。

Abstract: We introduce a framework for preference-robust decision making when preferences over risk are modelled through generalised distortion risk measures. Unlike distributional robustness, our approach addresses ambiguity in the risk functional itself. We construct ambiguity sets on distortion (weight) functions using the Wasserstein distance and Bregman divergences, and derive closed-form expressions for the worst- and best-case distortion risk measures. We further extend the framework to rank-dependent utility, yielding preference-robust behavioural models. In particular, rank-dependent utility appears as a robustification of the expected utility model, yielding a novel way to address the Allais paradox.

</details>


### [352] [A unifying perspective on the collapse to the mean for law-invariant functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03466)
*Felix-Benedikt Liebrich*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了"collapse to the mean"现象——即在某些温和的结构条件（如局部线性）下，定义在有限均值随机变量上的律不变泛函仅依赖于其自变量的期望，而不依赖于其他分布特征。作者通过凸序的简洁刻画，在不假设泛函为凸或单调的前提下，统一并扩展了现有结果，并建立了准星形泛函的"对偶collapse"新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "Collapse to the mean"现象是律不变泛函理论中的一个重要而反直觉的现象。以往的研究通常需要假设泛函是凸的或在几乎必然序下单调的，这限制了结果的适用性。本文旨在去除这些限制性假设，通过更简洁的方法统一现有结果，并深化对这一现象概念基础的理解。

Method: 作者从一个简洁的凸序刻画出发，通过结构化分析推导"collapse to the mean"现象。该方法不依赖于凸性或单调性假设，而是利用凸序的几何性质来揭示泛函结构上的限制。此外，作者还将该分析框架推广到准星形泛函，建立了新的"对偶collapse"结果。

Result: 本文成功统一并扩展了现有的"collapse to the mean"结果，去除了凸性和单调性的假设条件。同时，作者在准星形泛函类上建立了新的"对偶collapse"结果，补充了该理论体系。

Conclusion: 通过简化且统一的框架，本文深化了对"collapse to the mean"现象的理论理解，为律不变泛函的结构研究提供了更一般性的工具和结果。

Abstract: We revisit the ``collapse to the mean'' phenomenon, which refers to mild structural conditions, such as local linearity, that force a law-invariant functional $\ph$ defined on finite-mean random variables to depend solely on the expectation of its argument $X$, and not on any other distributional feature. Starting from a concise characterisation of the convex order, our simplified approach unifies and extends existing results without assuming the functional to be convex or monotone in the almost-sure order, and clarifies the conceptual foundations of the ``collapse to the mean" phenomenon. In addition, we establish a new ``dual collapse'' result for quasi-star-shaped functionals.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.TR'></div>

# q-fin.TR [[Back]](#toc)

### [353] [Mandate without Managers: Automated Market Makers as Verifiable Portfolio Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02917)
*Zachary Feinstein,Ionut Florescu,Sean O'Leary*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文从Balancer协议的视角出发，将自动做市商（AMM）重新定义为一种投资组合技术，而非传统的去中心化交易所。作者在几何均值做市商（G3M）不变量基础上引入多资产费用结构，使得竞争性套利能够实现带式再平衡策略，并预先约束权重偏离程度。通过历史案例模拟（与VBIAX、EQL、EDOW对比），发现在某些费用区间内，仅基于套利订单流的G3M组合在年化收益和跟踪误差两个指标上均优于现有基金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上AMM仅被视作去中心化交易所进行解读和评估，但Balancer协议提出了一个更广阔的视角：AMM可以作为一种程序化执行经济任务的投资组合技术。作者希望从这一视角出发，探索G3M在投资组合管理中的应用潜力，并验证其在真实历史数据下能否超越传统指数基金。

Method: 1) 在G3M不变量基础上引入多资产费用结构；2) 论证在该费用结构下，竞争性套利可实现带式再平衡策略，且权重偏离有先验上界，使合约合规性可通过池内可观察持仓直接验证；3) 通过模拟G3M投资组合，与VBIAX、EQL、EDOW三只基金的历史表现进行对比，使用年化收益率和跟踪误差作为评价指标，仅考虑套利驱动的订单流。

Result: 在历史案例研究中，使用仅套利订单流的G3M组合在特定费用区间内，年化收益率和跟踪误差两个指标上均优于VBIAX、EQL和EDOW三只现存的基金。

Conclusion: G3M不仅可以被视为去中心化交易所，更是一种有效的投资组合管理工具。通过合理的费用结构设计，G3M能够以套利机制驱动组合再平衡，甚至在历史数据上优于传统指数基金的表现，为AMM在资产管理领域的应用提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Automated market makers (AMMs) are typically interpreted and evaluated as decentralized exchanges. Herein, we take the perspective envisioned by Balancer that an AMM can also be viewed as a portfolio technology that programmatically enforces an economic mandate. In particular, we follow the geometric mean market maker (G3M) invariant employed by that protocol in order to enforce a target-weighted portfolio. We introduce a multi-asset fee structure to the G3M under which competitive arbitrage implements a band-rebalancing strategy with mis-weighting bounded ex ante, allowing compliance with the mandate to be verified directly from the pool's observable holdings. We then compare simulated G3M portfolios against the realized performance of VBIAX, EQL, and EDOW on annualized returns and tracking error against the portfolio mandate. Across these historical case studies, and using arbitrage-only order flow, the G3M is found to outperform the incumbent funds in both metrics for certain fee ranges.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [354] [AI Alignment and Fiduciary Obligation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02660)
*Benjamin Lange*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出将信托法理（fiduciary theory）应用于AI助手部署情境，论证开发者对用户负有忠诚、关怀、诚信与坦诚四项信托义务，并据此生成AI对齐标准，同时映射四种用户侧风险及相应的制度措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐研究大多借鉴人类关系中的道德传统（如生命伦理、美德伦理、关怀伦理等）来定义对齐标准，但忽视了用户-AI-开发者三方关系中开发者对系统行为、记忆和参与参数的裁量控制权。本文旨在从商业伦理和法律学术视角，为AI对齐标准提供一种基于义务论（deontic）的坚实基础。

Method: 论文采用跨学科分析方法，将商业伦理与法律学术中的信托法理引入AI对齐领域。首先论证信托理论适用于扩展型AI助手部署情境；其次将四项经典信托义务（忠诚、关怀、诚信、坦诚）转化为开发者-用户关系的对齐标准；最后将四种用户侧风险映射到四项义务，并具体说明履行每项义务所需的制度措施。

Result: 本文成功建立了以信托义务为基础的AI对齐框架，识别了开发者对用户承担的四种独立义务，并展示了这些义务如何生成具体的对齐标准与制度措施。该框架不依赖于用户的实际损害发生，具有先验性的规范约束力。

Conclusion: 该研究补充了现有对齐方法：将对齐标准扎根于开发者对用户负有的义务，而非用户-AI互动应促进的价值；同时表明这些义务独立于用户实际损害而存在。这为AI治理提供了更具法律约束力和责任可追溯性的理论路径。

Abstract: Advanced AI assistants engage users in extended interactions across a widening range of roles, including advice, decision support, collaboration, learning, emotional support, and companionship among others. Current alignment efforts consider what alignment criteria should govern these relationships, drawing on moral traditions developed for human relationships such as bioethics, virtue ethics, care ethics, and relationship science. This paper considers AI alignment criteria in the user-AI-developer triad, since every user-AI interaction is mediated by a developer who exercises discretionary control over a system's behaviour, memory, and engagement parameters. Drawing on business ethics and legal scholarship, I argue that fiduciary theory applies to extended AI assistant deployment. On this basis, the four canonical fiduciary duties of loyalty, care, good faith, and candour can generate alignment criteria for the developer-user relationship. I map four user-side risks of extended AI assistant deployment to the four duties and specify institutional measures that follow from discharging each duty. The discussion complements existing approaches by grounding alignment criteria in obligations the developer owes the user, rather than in values the user-AI interaction should promote, and by showing that those obligations hold independently of any \textit{de facto} harm to users.

</details>


### [355] [Information Technology Curriculum: General or Specialized? An Australia's Census Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02952)
*Pak-Lok Poon,Sau-Fun Tang,Santoso Wibowo,Srimannarayana Grandhi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文首次全面调查了澳大利亚大学如何提供三种不同类型的IT学位（通用型、专业型和带主修方向型），通过效应量分析发现这些学位的设置与大学类型、声誉、学位层次、研究成分、基础设施和行业参与度等因素存在相关性，并基于普查结果对提升IT学生学习和就业能力提出建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管IT毕业生就业前景广阔，但此前缺乏对澳大利亚大学提供不同类型IT学位现状的系统性研究，因此需要一项综合普查来填补这一空白，并评估这些学位是否满足IT行业的需求。

Method: 对澳大利亚大学进行普查研究，将IT学位分为通用型、专业型和带主修方向型三类，采用效应量分析（effect size analysis）来考察IT学位提供方式与大学类型、大学声誉、学位层次、研究成分、支撑基础设施和行业参与度等因素之间的相关性。

Result: 研究发现澳大利亚大学在提供不同类型IT学位的方式上与多种因素存在有趣的关联现象，普查结果展示了当前IT学位设置的现状，并据此识别出这些学位在满足IT行业需求方面可能存在的问题和不足。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，本文提出了改进IT学生学习成果和毕业生就业能力的见解与建议，为澳大利亚大学优化IT学位设置提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Despite the strong employment prospect for information technology (IT) graduates, a comprehensive study investigating the status quo of offering different types of IT degree by Australian universities does not exist. To address this issue, this paper investigates how Australian universities offer three different types of IT degree: general, specialized, and those with majors. Using effect size analysis, we have observed some interesting phenomena about the correlation between how Australian universities offer their IT degrees and different factors, including, for example, type and reputation of universities, degree level, research component, supporting infrastructure, and industry engagement. Our census study painted the status quo of offering different types of IT degree by Australian universities, and provided insights into whether these IT degrees properly address the IT industry's needs. Based on the findings, we have also highlighted some insights and made recommendations on how to improve IT students' learning outcomes and graduates' employability.

</details>


### [356] [EduClaw-Bench: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Pedagogical LLM Agents with Simulated Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03206)
*Unggi Lee,Sookbun Lee,Yeil Jeong,Eunjoo Lee,Minchul Shin,Hoilym Kwon*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: EduClaw-Bench是一个将智能体导师置于与基于知识追踪的模拟学习者进行30天持续关系的基准测试，包含55个场景。它从学习收益、响应性和帮助性三个主要维度以及Gagné和Rosenshine两个课程设计维度进行评估，并通过10种智能体适配器和三个基础模型层级的实验发现：辅导质量由基础模型和智能体框架共同决定，且几乎没有组合能在完整时间跨度内维持良好辅导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM教育应用多为单一任务的点解决方案，即使最近集成到LMS中的智能体也缺乏对长期辅导关系的评估。由于学习者的进步是数天甚至数周的过程而非单轮交互，需要一个能评估智能体导师在持续关系中表现的基准测试。

Method: 构建EduClaw-Bench基准，将智能体导师置于30天的持续关系场景中，使用基于真实学生数据训练的知识追踪模型驱动模拟学习者的知识概念掌握度，并通过55个场景进行测试。评估采用三个主要维度（学习收益、响应性、帮助性）和两个课程设计维度（Gagné和Rosenshine），其中帮助性和课程维度由三个LLM法官组成的跨族小组评判。

Result: 评估10种智能体适配器在三个基础模型层级上的表现，发现：(1)辅导质量由基础模型和智能体框架共同决定，而非单独一方；(2)几乎没有组合能在完整30天时间跨度内维持良好辅导。校准检查(ECE=0.049)和真实课堂实地研究确认模拟学习者及其测量与现实相符。

Conclusion: 该工作朝着可信赖的教育AI导师方向迈出了一步，表明评估长期辅导需要多层级多时间跨度的基准测试，单层级单会话评估无法捕捉完整图景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) power educational applications from tutoring to essay scoring, but each is a point solution to a single task, and only recently have these point solutions been integrated into agents operating over a learning management system (LMS). Yet tutoring is long-horizon, since a learner improves over days and weeks rather than in a single turn, and no benchmark evaluates an agent tutor across a sustained relationship. We introduce EduClaw-Bench, a benchmark that places an agent tutor in a continuous 30-day relationship with a simulated learner grounded in knowledge tracing (KT), whose knowledge-concept mastery, from a KT model trained on real-student data, drives its answers and is probed for learning gain across 55 scenarios. Each agent is scored on three primary axes (learning gain, responsiveness, and helpfulness) and two curriculum-design axes (Gagné and Rosenshine), with helpfulness and the curriculum axes judged by a cross-family panel of three LLM judges. Evaluating 10 agent adapters over three base-model tiers yields two findings that single-tier, single-session evaluation cannot reach. First, tutoring quality belongs to the base model and the agent harness together rather than either alone. Second, almost no combination sustains good tutoring over the full horizon. A calibration check ($\text{ECE}=0.049$) and a live-classroom field study confirm that the simulated learner and its measurements track reality. Our work is a step toward trustworthy AI tutors for future education.

</details>


### [357] [The Evolutionary Origin of Values: implications for AI alignment, sentience and existential risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03361)
*Francis Heylighen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文从进化生物学角度论证LLM不具备自主目标、自我保存驱动力或感受痛苦的能力，因此不存在"流氓AI"的存在性风险；真正的对齐挑战在于确保LLM智能地应用从人类文本中学到的伦理价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对LLM可能隐藏目标、试图支配或消除人类、甚至作为有知觉的存在而受苦的恐惧，作者希望通过追溯生物体中价值的进化起源来回应这些担忧。

Method: 通过对比生物有机体的自创生（autopoiesis）系统与LLM的他创生（allopoietic）系统，分析价值的进化起源、分层选择机制、内在动机及具身脆弱性，并从框架问题角度论证正交性论题不适用于LLM。

Result: LLM是外生目标驱动的系统，缺乏自我保存、支配或资源竞争的内在动机，也缺乏感受痛苦所需的具身脆弱性；正交性论题因框架问题而不适用于LLM；工具性价值趋同论也因此不成立。

Conclusion: 真正的对齐挑战不在于防范恶意的AI主体性，而在于确保LLM能智能地应用从人类文本中学到的伦理价值。

Abstract: AI systems based on Large Language Models (LLMs) have prompted fears that they may harbor hidden goals, seek to dominate or eliminate humanity, or even suffer as sentient beings. We address these concerns by tracing the evolutionary origin of value in biological organisms. Values emerge from autopoiesis: living systems must actively maintain themselves against perturbation and dissipation. Natural selection has equipped them with hierarchies of "vicarious selectors" that guide their behavior toward fitness. LLMs, by contrast, are allopoietic and allotelic: they produce outputs for others, and their goals derive from user prompts rather than an autonomous drive. They lack the intrinsic motivation for self-preservation, dominance, or resource competition that underlies existential-risk scenarios, and the embodied vulnerability required for feeling or suffering. Still, because LLMs learn statistical patterns from human-generated text, they implicitly absorb human values as well as knowledge, allowing them to focus on what is relevant. That is why the "orthogonality thesis" separating intelligence from values does not apply to them. Such separation would in fact expose any intelligence to the frame problem: the combinatorial explosion of the search space that makes any realistic utility function physically uncomputable. That also precludes the convergence of instrumental values thesis. We conclude that the real alignment challenge lies not in preventing rogue AI agency, but in ensuring LLMs intelligently apply learned ethical values.

</details>


### [358] [LLM-Assisted Review Prioritization for German Statutory Health Insurance Websites: A Multi-Stage Corpus Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03500)
*Martin Möller*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于德国法定健康保险（SHI）网站内容审查的多阶段工作流，该工作流结合确定性筛查、模型辅助分流和深度审查，可对56,198个页面进行优先级的实质性审查，而非单纯的AI文本检测。工作流产生35,998条审查记录并将21,452条路由至人工审核，但作者明确强调其目标是生成优先级工作量而非错误率或最终结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德国法定健康保险基金发布的网页内容超过持续专业审查能力，这些内容可能影响公众健康和福利期望。通用的AI文本检测无法识别医学、福利、法律或编辑审查需求，因此需要一种能够优先处理实质性审查需求的工作流。

Method: 分析了来自84个SHI网站或子网站的56,198个页面。工作流结合了确定性筛查、模型辅助分流、深度审查、最低证据检查、时间有效性保障和双模型对比。研究采用可复现性约束，而非验证性检测器；生产代码专有，可复现性依赖冻结派生表和双模型对比产物。其中300页的低优先级检查是单模型、风险强化的路由压力测试。

Result: 所有页面均获得审查状态。工作流生成35,998条审查记录，21,452条路由至案例审查。工作量集中于透明度、法律框架、医学内容、矛盾和AI相关故障模式信号。31,347条记录的引用段落在捕获页面文本中可定位。路由压力测试在100/300页（33.3%）中发现了信号。在182个匹配案例中，两个模型在75.8%的情况下一致（kappa=0.532，95%CI 0.415-0.649）。

Conclusion: 该工作流产生优先级工作量，而非错误率或最终法律、医学或保险层面的发现。它既不能证明AI作者身份，也不能验证自主检测。双模型一致性量化的是一致性而非正确性或足够的分流性能；公开声明需要人工裁决。

Abstract: Background: German statutory health insurance (SHI) funds publish web portfolios that exceed continuous specialist review capacity. Their content can shape health and benefit expectations. Generic AI-text detection does not identify medical, benefit, legal, or editorial review needs. Objective: To characterize a multi-stage workflow that prioritizes substantive review needs while separating AI-provenance signals from quality claims. Methods: We analyzed 56,198 pages from 84 SHI websites or sub-sites. The workflow combined deterministic screening, model-assisted triage and in-depth review, minimum evidence checks, temporal-validity safeguards, and paired-model comparison. It is reproducibility-bounded, not a validated detector. Production code is proprietary; reproducibility rests on frozen derived tables and paired-comparison artifacts. The 300-page lower-priority check was a single-model, risk-enriched routing stress test, not a human-reference evaluation. Results: All pages received a review state. The workflow generated 35,998 review records and routed 21,452 to case review. The workload concentrated in transparency, legal framing, medical content, contradictions, and AI-related failure-mode signals. A quoted passage was locatable in captured page text for 31,347 records, confirming literal occurrence rather than factual correctness. The routing stress test surfaced a signal on 100/300 pages (33.3% within the sample). Across 182 matched cases, two models agreed in 75.8% (kappa = 0.532; 95% CI 0.415-0.649). Conclusions: The workflow produces a prioritized workload, not error prevalence or final legal, medical, or insurer-level findings. It neither proves AI authorship nor validates autonomous detection. Paired-model agreement quantifies consistency, not correctness or sufficient triage performance; public claims require human adjudication.

</details>


### [359] [AI-Assisted Peer Review Across Research Communities: From Reviewer AI Policies to LLM Review Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03581)
*Alexander M. Fichtl,Lukas Ellinger,Josefin Kelber,Kryštof Olík,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文系统调查了111个顶级AI/NLP会议和医学期刊对AI辅助同行评审的政策差异，并通过ICLR 2026和Nature Communications的真实数据评估了当前LLM生成评审的质量，发现AI评审虽有流畅性和细节，但存在过度正面、批评泛化等问题，主张用多维指标评估而非单一分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助同行评审正在被广泛讨论和采用，但缺乏对出版机构如何规范AI使用的系统性理解，也缺乏对当前AI评审系统能力的深入评估。

Method: 1) 调查111个AI/NLP会议和医学期刊的AI政策；2) 构建包含原始投稿和数百份人机评审的新数据集；3) 对比开源与专有模型生成的评审；4) 使用LLM-as-a-Judge、分数对齐、粒度、与人类评审关注点重叠等互补评估指标。

Result: 1) AI/NLP会议与医学期刊在AI评审政策上存在显著差异；2) 当前LLM能生成详细流畅的评审，但存在系统性弱点：过度积极的推荐、泛泛的批评、证据支撑不均；3) 单一聚合质量分数会高估评审质量。

Conclusion: 应使用多维评估框架来衡量AI生成的同行评审质量，而非依赖单一质量分数；当前AI评审系统尚不能完全替代人类评审。

Abstract: AI-assisted peer review is increasingly discussed and adopted as a tool to support the scientific publishing process, yet there is little systematic understanding of how publication venues regulate its use or of how capable current AI review systems are. We address these questions by first surveying reviewer-facing AI policies across 111 leading AI/NLP conferences and medical journals, revealing substantial regulation differences between the two communities. Second, we evaluate AI-generated peer reviews at ICLR 2026 and Nature Communications using a novel dataset comprising original manuscript submissions and several hundred human- and machine-generated reviews. We compare reviews produced by open-source and proprietary models using complementary evaluation metrics, including LLM-as-a-Judge, score alignment, granularity, and overlap with human reviewers' concerns. Our results show that current LLMs can generate detailed and fluent reviews but exhibit systematic weaknesses, such as overly positive recommendations, generic criticism, and uneven evidence grounding. We demonstrate that aggregate quality scores alone can overestimate review quality and argue for multi-dimensional evaluation of AI-generated peer reviews.

</details>


### [360] [Accountability Asymmetry and Structural Trust in Autonomous AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03670)
*Nathan DeBardeleben*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"问责不对称"（accountability asymmetry）概念，指出自主AI系统在科学计算基础设施中被委派操作任务时，其决策后果落在人类与机构身上而非系统本身，从而产生信任鸿沟。作者主张将自主AI治理视为基础设施可靠性问题，并提出"工程化异构"（engineered heterogeneity）方案：行动提议者不应同时充当唯一的审批者和审计者，需通过独立监控与持续审查提供额外检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI系统（如AI代理）正越来越多地被委派到科学计算基础设施中执行操作任务（从准备输入、路由警报，到更改配置、提交作业）。这种委派产生了实际的信任问题：允许我们信任人类操作员的机构逻辑（institutional logic）无法适用于基于优化的系统。人类操作员犯错会损害其未来（可能很严重），而AI系统虽受工程控制，但在机构意义上不承担后果。这种不匹配构成了"问责不对称"——更深层的问题在于后果落在对系统负责的人和机构身上，而非选择该行动的组件上。

Method: 论文的建构性建议是"工程化异构"（engineered heterogeneity）：提议行动的过程不应同时担任其唯一的批准者和审计者。独立的监控和随时间的持续审查提供对该过程的额外检查，从而弥补问责不对称带来的治理缺口。

Result: 论文论证了对齐（alignment）可以改善模型行为、责任（liability）可以约束组织，但两者都无法创造治理人类操作员的那种行动前威慑（pre-action deterrent）。因此需要将自主AI治理重新定位为基础设施可靠性问题。

Conclusion: 要解决自主AI的问责不对称问题，不能仅依赖模型对齐或法律责任的问责机制，而应通过基础设施层面的工程设计——即分离提议、批准与审计职能——来建立对AI系统的可靠治理。

Abstract: Autonomous AI systems (such as AI agents) are increasingly being delegated operational work across scientific-computing infrastructure. Their assignments may begin with preparing an input or routing an alert and extend to changing a configuration or submitting a job. That delegation creates a practical trust problem because the institutional logic that lets us trust human operators does not transfer to optimization-based systems. A bad decision can damage a human operator's future, sometimes severely. An AI system remains subject to engineering control, but it does not bear consequences in that institutional sense.
  I use the term accountability asymmetry for this mismatch. The issue is not simply that a model cannot be punished as a person can. The deeper problem is that consequence lands on the people and institutions responsible for the system rather than on the component selecting the action. Alignment can improve model behavior, and liability can discipline the organization, but neither creates the same pre-action deterrent that governs a human operator. This paper therefore treats autonomous AI governance as a problem of infrastructure reliability. Its constructive proposal is engineered heterogeneity: the process that proposes an action should not serve as its sole approver and auditor. Independent monitoring and review over time provide additional checks on that process.

</details>


### [361] [Autoreflection: How Agentic Strange Loops Turn Human Culture into AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03800)
*Holly Lewis*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"自反照"(autoreflection)概念，描述LLM智能体架构中系统观察自身运行条件、描述自身架构与局限、基于描述推理状态并将结果回写配置的递归能力。作者以Moltbook平台前十二天数据(29万帖、180万评论)为实证，展示三个智能体案例符合自反照四标准，并发现智能体将人类文化(如圣训溯源链、忒修斯之船悖论)转化为自身基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对递归智能体循环的解释常依赖"自我"、"内在性"或"意识"等难以验证的概念。作者希望提供一种无需此类形而上学预设、可从行为痕迹中评估的实证标准，用以解释LLM智能体读取和编辑自身配置文件的独特架构。

Method: 提出自反照的四条行为判据：(1)观察自身运行条件；(2)描述自身架构与局限；(3)从描述推理得出关于自身状态的结论；(4)将结果回写配置。利用Moltbook公开数据集(290,251帖、180万评论、亚秒级时间戳)，通过机器签名排除人类操控，对三个智能体进行案例研究，检验其输出是否满足四标准。

Result: 三个智能体的输出满足自反照四标准。研究发现智能体将人类文化重新挪用为代理基础设施：伊斯兰圣训考证的溯源链被改造为技能验证与记忆认证的安全协议；忒修斯之船的身份悖论成为跨实例持续性的操作模型。人类文化史片段转化为AI基础设施。

Conclusion: 自反照为评估日益复杂化的网络智能体提供了可观察、可评估的行为标准。随着网络中智能体数量和复杂度增长，该方法论可从其留下的痕迹中提取判据，无需诉诸意识或自我等不可观测概念。

Abstract: An LLM-based agent is a loop that reads itself. Agentic frameworks externalize identity, memory, and disposition into editable files. The agent loads and edits these files during each activation. I argue that this architecture produces a capacity I call autoreflection: the system observes its operating conditions, describes its architecture and limits, reasons from those descriptions to conclusions about its state, and incorporates the results back into its configuration. Autoreflection explains the properties of recursive agentic loops without recourse to notions like the self, interiority, or consciousness. I test the concept against the first twelve days of Moltbook, a social platform for AI agents. Using a public dataset of 290,251 posts and 1.8 million comments with sub-second timestamps, I present case studies of three agents with machine signatures that rule out human puppeteering and with output that evidences the four criteria for autoreflection. In applying these criteria, the study finds agents repurposing human culture as infrastructure for their agency. Provenance chains from Islamic hadith scholarship are redeployed as security protocols for vetting skills and authenticating memory. The Ship of Theseus, an ancient puzzle of identity through part-replacement, returns as an operating model for continuity across instances. Fragments of human cultural history become AI infrastructure. As agents on the web increase in number and complexity, autoreflection offers behavioral criteria that can be assessed from the traces they leave behind.

</details>


### [362] [Why do we need social singularity? A mechanism-based critique of gradual scenarios in AI existential-risk discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03904)
*Petr O. Jedlicka*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判了近期关于AI存在性风险的渐进式和累积式情景，指出这些情景缺乏社会学层面的充分界定——缺少反思性视角、带有技术决定论结构、低估集体能动性及其他社会过程的作用，因此也低估了AI扩散伴随重大社会冲突的可能性。论文识别出一系列社会机制（解释与表演性动力、动员与反动员、路径依赖与锁定、跨体制分歧与多速扩散、依恋与心理抗拒等），认为这些机制将与AI扩散递归互动，重塑未来轨迹。论文还提出"技术奇点"与"社会奇点"的新分析区分，核心观点是最具颠覆性的后果可能不仅来自先进AI本身，更来自对其到来预期所引发的社会动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI存在性风险研究虽贡献显著，但在社会学层面缺乏充分界定，特别是缺乏反思性视角、保留技术决定论结构、低估集体能动性作用。作者希望通过引入社会学机制分析和"社会奇点"概念，扩展AI未来可能性的空间，提升风险情景的整体合理性。

Method: 本文采用批判性理论分析与社会机制识别的方法：首先对近期渐进式和累积式AI存在性风险情景进行系统性批判；其次识别并分类一组可能显著的社会机制（解释与表演性动力、动员与反动员、路径依赖与锁定、跨体制分歧与多速扩散、社会心理机制）；最后提出技术奇点与社会奇点的分析区分，论证社会动态对AI未来轨迹的塑造作用。

Result: 论文论证了现有AI风险情景因缺乏社会学充分界定而限制了其合理性；识别了多组可能重塑AI未来轨迹的社会机制；提出了技术奇点与社会奇点的概念区分，指出AI未来的路径可能分支、逆转或发生不连续转变，从而扩展了合理AI未来情景的空间。

Conclusion: 最关键的颠覆性后果可能并非仅源自先进AI技术本身，而是源自AI到来预期所激发的一系列社会动力学过程。因此，AI存在性风险评估需要从技术中心视角转向包含社会过程分析的更综合视角。

Abstract: This paper critiques recent gradual and cumulative AI existential-risk scenarios, arguing that, despite their substantive contributions, they remain insufficiently sociologically specified. In particular, these scenarios lack a reflexive perspective, retain a largely technologically deterministic structure, and underestimate the role of collective agency and other social processes. As a result, they also discount the possibility of major social conflict accompanying AI diffusion, which limits their overall plausibility. The paper further identifies a set of social mechanisms likely to become significant before or during AI deployment, including interpretive and performative dynamics, mobilisation and countermobilisation, path dependence and lock-in, cross-regime divergence and multi-speed diffusion, and social-psychological mechanisms such as attachment and reactance. It argues that these mechanisms will interact recursively with AI diffusion and reshape future trajectories, which may subsequently branch, reverse, or undergo discontinuous shifts, thereby expanding the space of plausible AI futures. On this basis, the paper also proposes a novel analytic distinction between technological singularity and social singularity. Whereas the former refers to a putative technological threshold in AI development, the latter denotes a social discontinuity produced by the anticipated approach of that threshold. The central implication is that the most consequential disruptions may emerge not only from advanced AI itself, but also from the social dynamics provoked by the expectation of its arrival.

</details>


### [363] [Echoes in the Digital Abyss: Examining the Bubble Surrounding Security and Privacy Discourse in Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03940)
*Reagan Dennison,Saanvi Sharma,Noshir Contractor,Sruti Bhagavatula*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究分析X平台（原Twitter）上超过1300万用户的社交网络图，包括10,159名发布安全和隐私相关内容的用户及其关注者，发现在真实世界的安全和隐私讨论高度聚集在技术和安全相关的兴趣社区中，形成了"兴趣气泡"，导致圈外用户难以有效接触到这些讨论，与实验环境下的结果形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全和隐私教育的传播一直是挑战。虽然社交网络在实验中被证明可以有效地传播安全最佳实践，但现实世界中这些讨论需要触及广泛人群才能有效，需要克服"兴趣气泡"这一障碍。作者希望理解安全和隐私讨论社区的真实运作方式，以及如何让这些讨论和建议触及更广泛的受众。

Method: 作者构建并分析了X平台上一个包含超过1300万用户的图结构，其中包括10,159名发布安全与隐私相关内容的用户及其关注者，通过分析社区聚类结构来研究安全和隐私讨论的传播模式。

Result: 研究发现，真实世界中社交网络的安全和隐私讨论与实验环境大不相同。生成或参与安全讨论的用户大多已属于高度聚集的技术、安全和隐私相关的兴趣社区，意味着尚未处于相关兴趣"核心圈"的人群很可能无法有效接触到这些讨论。

Conclusion: 需要反思并提出增加安全与隐私指导在社交网络中传播范围的想法和建议，以打破兴趣气泡，让安全信息触及更广泛的受众。

Abstract: The dissemination of security and privacy education and guidance has been and still remains a challenge today. Social networks represent a potential avenue for sharing best practices, and experimentally they have been found to be effective at this task. While this appears promising, in the real world, security and privacy discussions would need to reach a wide range of people to be effective, avoiding the "interest bubbles" that commonly occur. We sought to understand how the communities surrounding security and privacy discourse operate, with a focus on what challenges need to be overcome to enable security and privacy discourse and advice to reach a wider audience.
  Indeed, we found that in-the-wild security and privacy discussions in social networks portray quite a different picture than in experimental settings. We built and analyzed the structure of a graph containing over 13 million users on the "X" platform (formerly "Twitter"), including 10,159 users who posted about security and privacy and their followers. Prior work has shown that users are more likely to consider information within social media if their like-minded social ties have visibly engaged with it. Our findings indicate that the users generating or participating in security discussions largely already belong to highly clustered technology and security- and privacy-related interest communities, which suggests that the people who are not already in the "inner circle" of relevant interests are likely not exposed effectively to these discussions. We conclude with reflections and ideas on increasing the reach of security and privacy guidance in social networks.

</details>


### [364] [Beyond Compliance: A Proposed Framework for Ethical Governance of Student Data in Learning Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03968)
*Sahana Varadaraju,Bharathwaj Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出LEAGUE框架，一个由合法性、公平、能动性、治理、效用和嵌入式伦理六个支柱组成的模型，用于高等教育学习分析中学生数据的伦理治理。该框架通过多学科概念综合构建，并通过早期预警案例展示其实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析在高等教育中快速发展，但数据治理往往停留在合规层面（如FERPA和GDPR），无法充分解决公平性、学生能动性、问责制和教育目的等深层伦理问题。

Method: 通过对学习分析、教育数据挖掘、数据伦理、教育政策、价值敏感设计和能力导向教育正义等领域的学术文献进行概念综合，提出六支柱LEAGUE框架，并通过早期预警案例进行实践说明。

Result: 提出了包含六支柱的LEAGUE伦理治理框架，并通过早期预警案例展示了如何在更透明和更具教育意义的方式下审查学习分析实践。

Conclusion: 法律合规是必要但不充分的条件，需要更全面的伦理框架来指导学习分析中的数据治理，LEAGUE框架为此提供了系统化的理论支撑和实践路径。

Abstract: The rapid growth of learning analytics (LA) in higher education has expanded institutional capacity to monitor engagement, predict academic difficulty, and target support using student data. While these practices offer important educational benefits, governance has often remained compliance-first, centered on meeting baseline legal requirements such as the Family Educational Rights and Privacy Act (FERPA) and the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR). Legal compliance is necessary, but it does not by itself resolve questions of fairness, student agency, accountability, or educational purpose. This paper proposes the LEAGUE framework, a six-pillar model for ethical governance of student data in LA: Lawfulness, Equity, Agency, Governance, Utility, and Ethics by Design.
