The framework is developed through a conceptual synthesis of scholarship in learning analytics, educational data mining, data ethics, educational policy, value-sensitive design, and capability-oriented approaches to educational justice. Its practical value is demonstrated through an illustrative early alert case that shows how institutions can review learning analytics in a more transparent and educationally meaningful way.

</details>


### [365] [When AI Wears Many Hats: The Role of Generative Artificial Intelligence in Marketing Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03973)
*Unnati Narang,Vishal Sachdev,Ruichun Liu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过多角度分析（课程大纲审查、教育者调查和定性访谈），基于角色理论和探究社区（CoI）模型，提出了生成式AI在营销教育中可扮演的三种角色：导师（tutor）、队友（teammate）和工具（tool），并探讨了每种角色对教学、社交和认知存在感的不同影响，同时指出了数据隐私、抄袭、AI依赖和评估公平性等伦理挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究指出了生成式AI融入教育的机遇与风险，但目前缺乏一个全面的框架来指导生成式AI在营销教育中的有效使用，特别是如何系统地理解其角色定位及其对学习体验的影响。

Method: 采用多管齐下的分析方法，包括：(1) 审查营销课程教学大纲；(2) 对营销教育者进行问卷调查；(3) 进行后续定性访谈。在此基础上，结合角色理论（Role Theory）和探究社区（CoI）模型构建理论框架。

Result: 提出生成式AI在营销教育中可以扮演导师、队友和工具三种角色，每种角色对教学存在感、社交存在感和认知存在感产生不同影响。例如，作为导师可帮助学生掌握理论概念，作为队友可通过支持头脑风暴和问题解决促进协作。同时识别了数据隐私、抄袭、AI依赖和评估公平性等伦理问题。

Conclusion: 研究为生成式AI在营销课程中的谨慎整合提供了具体示例，并对营销教育者、学习者和政策制定者提出了实践启示，旨在培养适应职场需求的营销毕业生。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GAI) is increasingly being integrated into marketing education and is reshaping the skillsets required in marketing careers. While research has highlighted the promise and perils of incorporating GAI into education, there remains a need for a comprehensive framework to guide its effective use. In this research, we conduct a multipronged analysis, including a review of marketing course syllabi, a survey of marketing educators, and follow-up qualitative interviews. Building on Role Theory and the Community of Inquiry (CoI) model, we propose that GAI can assume three roles in marketing education: tutor, teammate, and tool. Each role influences teaching, social, and cognitive presence differently, shaping the learning experience and preparing workplace-ready marketing graduates. For instance, as a tutor, GAI can aid students in grasping theoretical concepts, while as a teammate, it can foster collaboration by supporting brainstorming and problem-solving activities. However, ethical considerations such as data privacy, plagiarism, dependency on AI, and fairness in assessment must be addressed to ensure its responsible adoption in marketing education. We provide concrete examples for GAI's careful integration in marketing courses, and its implications for marketing educators, learners, and policymakers.

</details>

### [366] [Option Pricing with Time-Changed Fractional Brownian Motion: A Fractional Variance Gamma Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03925)
*Robert Jarrow,Jayen Tan*

Main category: q-fin.MF

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时间变换的分数布朗运动（fBm），通过在Gamma随机活动时间（累积执行交易时间）下评估fBm，恢复了半鞅结构，并以此构建了分数Variance Gamma（fVG）模型用于期权定价。通过对标普500的实证分析，估计的Hurst指数约为0.45，表明收益矩呈现轻度次线性时间缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fBm虽具有长程依赖、路径粗糙和异常扩散等吸引人的金融建模特征，但其非半鞅性质使其无法使用传统的无套利期权定价方法。作者旨在克服这一根本性限制。

Method: 将fBm在随机Gamma活动时间（代表累积执行交易时间）下进行评估，获得时间变换的fBm，保留fBm的原始特性同时恢复半鞅结构。在此基础上构建分数Variance Gamma（fVG）模型，并提出广义矩方法（GMM）估计程序用于期权定价。

Result: 对标普500指数的实证分析表明，估计的Hurst指数约为0.45，与收益矩的轻度次线性时间缩放一致。

Conclusion: 时间变换构造有效解决了fBm非半鞅带来的定价困难，fVG模型为包含长程依赖特征的金融资产提供了可行的期权定价框架，实证结果支持该模型对真实市场数据的适用性。

Abstract: Fractional Brownian motion (fBm) exhibits attractive features for financial modeling, including long-range dependence, path roughness, and anomalous diffusion. However, its non-semimartingale nature precludes the use of conventional no-arbitrage approaches to option pricing. We address this limitation by introducing a time-changed fBm, obtained by evaluating fBm at stochastic gamma activity time, where activity time represents cumulative executed trading time. The resulting process retains the defining properties of fBm while recovering the semimartingale structure. Building on this construction, we develop the fractional Variance Gamma (fVG) model and propose a generalized method of moments (GMM) estimation procedure for option pricing. An empirical analysis of the S\&P 500 yields an estimated Hurst exponent of approximately 0.45, consistent with mildly sublinear temporal scaling of return moments.

</details>
